No per-play `AiDecisionTrace` data was available, so the decision note treats its split as proxy classification rather than definitive guard-state proof: `guard_never_reached_hand_above_cap` (15/26, 57.7%) and `guard_never_reached_board_below_floor` (11/26, 42.3%). No single narrow fix is supported by the available artifact because there are two proxy modes, neither exceeds 60%, and no exception-based pattern can be proven. - Completed repair phase: `cFp41.1` Recursive Scoring Guard For Expanded Benchmarks (`docs/spec/2026-05-19-cFp41.1-specs.md`, `audit/reports/2026-05-19-cFp41.1-report.md`). Repairs the `legal-heuristic-v1` stack overflow found by the cFp41 expanded starter-matrix run: `benchmark-v1-starter-matrix-expanded-v1`, matchup `starter-nilfgaard-heuristic-v1-vs-monsters-heuristic-v0`, seed `starter-matrix-expanded-010`, mirror index `0`, `policy_select_failed`, `Maximum call stack size exceeded`. Adds `npm run benchmark:long` as a user-run orchestration wrapper around existing benchmark npm commands. - Latest Cluster F AI-behavior implementation: `cFp39` Medic No-Target Timing Guard (`docs/spec/2026-05-18-cFp39-specs.md`, `audit/reports/2026-05-18-cFp39-report.md`). Adds a narrow no-target Medic delay guard: `MEDIC_NO_TARGET_DELAY_PENALTY = 260` is applied in `scorePlayMove` when the move is a no-target Medic source play (zero revive candidates), a useful non-Medic line exists, and no tactical exception (last-gem catch-up, match-winning, round-3 low-hand, opponent-passed, only-play, no-useful-alternative) applies. Implements `isNoTargetMedicSourcePlay`, `hasUsefulNonMedicLine`, and `shouldApplyNoTargetMedicDelayPenalty` helpers in `legalHeuristicPolicyV1.ts`. Extends `AiDecisionMedicTimingAnalysis` with three new hidden-info-safe booleans (`selectedNoTargetMedicDelayRisk`, `betterNonMedicAlternativeAvailable`, `noTargetMedicDelayPenaltyApplied`). Updates `explainLegalHeuristicV1Decision.ts` with cFp39 reason strings. Medic scores are conditionally adjusted (+260) in round-investment `positiveUnitSourceCardIds` computation only when the cFp39 penalty actually applies. A Medic-specific clear-round-benefit exception in `shouldPassForRoundInvestment` is placed before `shouldPassForResourceExhaustion` so emergency Medic tempo plays cannot be blocked by resource exhaustion. Added 10 focused cFp39 tests (8 original + 2 repair regression tests for conditional compensation). AI Lab metadata updated to cFp39 and refreshed by cFp40.1. Benchmark: v1 smoke 10/2/0 vs v0; v1 starter matrix 104/14/2 vs v0; failure mining 296 findings, medic_timing_risk reduced from 5 to 3. No engine rules, legal moves, UI behavior, catalog data, deck presets, benchmark suite definitions, or v0 behavior changed. Note: cFp39 is no longer the latest AI-behavior — cFp43 is now the latest completed implementation.
- Completed phase: `cFp37` Pass Decision Alignment And Resource Gate Scope-Down (`docs/spec/2026-05-18-cFp37-specs.md`, `audit/reports/2026-05-18-cFp37-report.md`). Behavior repair for the cFp36 round-resource budget gate regression. Introduces `shouldPassForResourceExhaustion` helper in `legalHeuristicPolicyV1.ts` with 10 hard-block conditions: resource gate returns true only when `resourceExhaustionRecommended === true`, candidate is play_card, round < 3, ownGems > 1, opponent not passed, not card-advantage, recommendation is `preserve_future_hand` or `sacrifice_round`, and no single-move catch-up with upper bound can win. Blocks pass when recommendation is `continue` or `fight_last_gem`. Updates `explainLegalHeuristicV1Decision.ts` with cFp37-appropriate reason strings for blocked resource pressure. Adds 9 focused cFp37 tests in `engineAiPolicy.test.ts` (7 original + 3 new - 1 duplicate after repairs; repairs also corrected audit report test-count math, added the missing `single-move catch-up` reason string, strengthened use-leader/catch-up assertions, and updated AI Lab metadata). Benchmark: v1 smoke 10/2/0 vs v0 (unchanged); v1 starter matrix 102/16/2 vs v0 (restored to cFp35 baseline from 89/29/2 at repaired cFp36); failure mining 295 findings, suspicious_pass = 222 (restored from 318 at repaired cFp36). Repeat-run artifact hashes stable.
- Completed phase: `cFp36.1` Benchmark Regression Review (`docs/spec/2026-05-18-cFp36.1-specs.md`, `docs/research/literature/ai/decisions/2026-05-18-cfp36.1-regression-review.md`, `audit/reports/2026-05-18-cFp36.1-report.md`). Analysis-only phase comparing cFp35 baseline artifacts at `1c3f634` against repaired cFp36 artifacts. - Completed phase: `cFp36` Round Resource Exhaustion Tuning with repair (`docs/spec/2026-05-17-cFp36-specs.md`, `audit/reports/2026-05-17-cFp36-report.md`). Adds a conservative non-elimination round resource budget gate to `legal-heuristic-v1`. The budget is based on future-hand quality (healthy=5, thin=4, poor=3 own board cards), round number (round 2 adjustment for gem advantage, round 3 excluded), and score delta. Preserves card-advantage/Spy, useful leader, last-gem, match-winning, single-card-hand, and prompt-resolution exceptions. Extends hidden-info-safe `AiDecisionRoundInvestmentAnalysis` with `roundResourceBudget`, `roundResourcePressure`, `resourceExhaustionRecommended`, and `resourceExhaustionReason`. Updated explanation text with cFp36 reason strings. Extended product diagnostic scanner with cFp36 safe enums. Repair: fixed `buildRoundResourcePressure` (under-budget returns "none"), separated exception reasons (resourceExhaustionRecommended=false) from budget-exceeded reasons (resourceExhaustionRecommended=true), added match-winning and cheap-catch-up exceptions, narrowed play-card exception explanation branch to only handle real cFp36 reasons (falls through to best-move/weathered logic when reason is "none"). Benchmark totals: v1 smoke 10/2/0 vs v0 (unchanged); v1 starter matrix — cFp35 origin-dev baseline: 102/16/2, pre-repair cFp36 branch: 88/30/2, repaired cFp36 branch: 89/29/2 (120 records); failure mining 397 findings, `round_resource_exhaustion` still rank 1 in tuning queue.

Calibrates the cFp34 failure-mining evaluator without changing `legal-heuristic-v1`, v0, engine rules, legal moves, UI behavior, catalog data, deck presets, or H100/Slurm workflow. `suspicious_pass` is no longer emitted solely because `stopLossRecommended === false`; the refreshed artifact has 120 records and 295 findings, with `suspicious_pass` reduced from 315 to 222 and 93 broad pass candidates recorded only as aggregate suppressed analyzer-noise counts (`preserve_future_hand_pass` 68, `stop_loss_pass` 24, `insufficient_context` 1). The failure-mining artifact bundle now includes `tuning-queue.md`; its ranked cFp36 candidates are `round_resource_exhaustion`, `weathered_row_low_tempo`, `medic_no_target_timing`, `skellige_matchup_skew`, and `remaining_suspicious_pass`, with cFp36 recommended to tune round-resource exhaustion first rather than broad suspicious-pass behavior.

- Completed phase: `cCp32.2` Scoia'tael Mulligan Presentation Repair (`docs/spec/2026-05-16-cCp32.2-specs.md`, `audit/reports/2026-05-16-cCp32.2-report.md`). Restores the AI mulligan exit presentation in exact-one Scoia'tael matches before the post-mulligan first-player prompt resolves, covering keep-hand, one-redraw, and two-redraw animation paths. - Completed phase: `cCp32.1` Scoia'tael Post-Mulligan First-Player Choice (implementation + repair complete; specs `docs/spec/2026-05-16-cCp32.1-specs.md` and `docs/spec/2026-05-16-cCp32.1-repair-specs.md`; report `audit/reports/2026-05-16-cCp32.1-report.md`). cCp32.1 corrects cCp32's pre-game timing by moving Scoia'tael first-player choice to a post-mulligan engine prompt. Removes `ScoiataelFirstPlayerChoice` from `MatchConfig`, removes `scoiataelFirstPlayerChoice` from `StartEngineMatchOptions`, strips pre-game scoia'tael selector from `AuthenticPreGameScreen.tsx` and `preGameViewModel.ts`, and adds a `scoiatael_first_player_choice` prompt that opens after both mulligans complete when exactly one seat plays Scoia'tael. The repair makes the prompt visible and actionable in the product match UI while `phase === "mulligan"`, rewrites the stale browser smoke from pre-game selector coverage to post-mulligan prompt coverage, gives v0/v1 deterministic `self` prompt fallback, adds public AI observation fields for future mulligan strategy (`ownHasPostMulliganFirstPlayerChoice`, `opponentHasPostMulliganFirstPlayerChoice`), updates rules/product/AI docs, and records benchmark status. Resolving the prompt with `"self"` or `"opponent"` emits `faction_ability_resolved` (policy: `post_mulligan_choice`), `turn_set` (reason: `scoiatael_override`), and transitions to `playing` phase. - Completed phase: `cFp32` Legal-Heuristic-v1 Stop-Loss Round Sacrifice (`docs/spec/2026-05-15-cFp32-specs.md`, `audit/reports/2026-05-15-cFp32-report.md`). cFp32 adds a stop-loss gate that triggers `sacrifice_round` for non-elimination rounds where catch-up is publicly impossible or too expensive. - Completed phase: `cFp38` Weathered Row Low-Tempo Tuning (`docs/spec/2026-05-18-cFp38-specs.md`, `audit/reports/2026-05-18-cFp38-report.md`). Adds a narrow scoring penalty to `legal-heuristic-v1` for spending meaningful non-hero units (printed strength ≥ 6) into the AI's own weathered rows where effective strength collapses to ≤ 3, when a clearly better legal line exists. Implements `isOwnWeatheredLowTempoUnitPlacement`, `hasClearlyBetterNonWeatheredLine`, and `shouldExemptFromWeatheredLowTempoPenalty` helpers. Penalty formula: `180 + lostStrength * 22`. Tactical exceptions preserve Spy/card-advantage, strong Medic revive, Muster/linked callers, match-winning plays, last-gem catch-up, and no-better-line cases. Updates `AiDecisionWeatherPlacementAnalysis` with three new hidden-info-safe booleans (`selectedMoveLowTempoWeatherRisk`, `betterNonWeatheredAlternativeAvailable`, `weatheredLowTempoPenaltyApplied`). Updated `explainLegalHeuristicV1Decision.ts` with corrected cFp38 reason strings. Added 11 focused cFp38 tests (Repair 1: fixed unit-kind guard to `card.kind === "unit"`, fixed `betterNonWeatheredAlternativeAvailable` semantics, fixed reason string logic, strengthened test coverage with v0 isolation and spy exception). Repair 2: strengthened exception tests (muster, Medic) to prove exemption alongside `hasClearlyBetterNonWeatheredLine=true`; strengthened v0 isolation test (test 10) with real behavioral fixture — weathered 15 vs clean 7 on fog; all exported helpers now used in tests instead of shadow logic. Repair 1–3 are all tests-and-docs-only — no scoring behavior change. Repair 3 strengthened Test 2 (weathered 20 vs clean 7 proves cFp38 alone flips selection), strengthened Spy exemption test with clearly-better clean alternative, corrected report reason-string wording. Benchmark: v1 smoke 10/2/0 vs v0 (unchanged); v1 starter matrix 102/16/2 vs v0 (unchanged from cFp37); failure-mining 295 findings, weathered_row_play = 21 (unchanged from cFp37). Repeat-run artifact hashes stable. cFp38 is the latest completed AI-behavior implementation.

- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-05-cEp13-report.md` (cEp13). Adds generated weather row overlays to the authentic match table before the next AI phase without changing engine rules, scoring, legal-move generation, command execution, Redux adapter behavior, AI policy, catalog data, deck builder, Card Studio, route defaults, or legacy UI. Project-bound overlay assets live under `public/images/weather-overlays/` and are derived from the approved Imagegen concept sheet. `buildWeatherRowOverlayViewModel(...)` maps public weather-zone cards to row effects: Frost marks close rows, Fog marks ranged rows, Rain marks siege rows, and Skellige Storm marks ranged plus siege rows with a distinct teal/violet storm treatment; Clear Weather is ignored. `AuthenticMatchScreen.tsx` renders those overlays behind row contents and legal target controls using stable `data-weather-overlay` / `data-weather-effect` attributes, preserving click targeting, drag/drop, right-click inspection, and hidden-info safety. The latest cEp13 asset iteration uses user-converted SVG runtime overlays at `0.8` opacity, preserves light/white row label, row score, empty-state text, and reserved horn-slot icon/border contrast for active weather rows, removes visible row/weather target buttons and board-card `choose` badges, makes legal board rows and the highlighted Weather panel accept click/drop directly, aims broad-target card-flight at the actual row/weather card strip insertion area, renders weather-affected unit strength text red from score modifiers, replaces visible Commander's Horn target labels with icon-only reserved horn slots that still carry exact legal click/drop move ids when selected, with placed horn slots sized 8px wider and 8px taller than the card inside, renders leader status state/category/reason in one inline score-card row without duplicated non-passed `active` copy, replaces score-card `gems N` text with two 30px cropped transparent life-gem icons per player underneath the faction name from `public/images/ui/life-gem.png` / `life-gem-broken.png`, moves hand/deck counts below those gems with darker code-native card icons, bold 16px counts underneath each icon, resource columns aligned under the two life gems, and no duplicated discard count, turns the battle log into a hidden-scrollbar full-match command/event feed that auto-scrolls to the newest entry with number/type/message rendered as separate columns, move-only enumeration, red/accent move message text at the normal log font size, matching number/type label sizing, and quieter unnumbered event rows, applies the same hidden-scrollbar/fade-edge scroll treatment to bounded pre-game/deck-builder/Card Studio list regions while grouping `open deck builder →` beside `card studio →` in the pre-game top bar and making the deck-builder and Card Studio workspaces span the viewport instead of sitting in centered table frames, and makes one-card mulligan selection match in-game hand selection by replacing the selected card when another mulligan card is clicked.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-05-cEp12-report.md` (cEp12). Adds product drag/drop and card-flight polish to the authentic match table without changing engine rules, legal-move generation, command execution, Redux adapter behavior, AI policy, catalog data, deck builder, Card Studio, route defaults, or legacy UI. `buildSelectedCardDragTargetViewModel(...)` derives drag-eligible move ids and drag-specific hint copy only from the existing cEp11 selected-card spatial target model: board rows, visible board-card targets, row-horn slots, and the Weather panel; fallback/no-target actions remain right-rail buttons and are not draggable. `AuthenticMatchScreen.tsx` now lets only visible playable human hand cards begin a pointer drag after a small movement threshold, keeps normal click selection and right-click inspection intact, ignores touch dragging for now, dispatches only exact legal `PlayCard` move ids carried by existing target buttons, and treats invalid drops as no-ops. Legal targets expose presentation-only `data-drop-move-id` / `data-drop-state`, hand cards expose `data-drag-state`, and successful human hand plays render a transient source-to-target card-flight overlay while dispatching the engine command immediately. AI hidden-origin movement never animates from hidden locations. CSS adds active drop highlighting, drag preview, card-flight, and reduced-motion handling inside the existing authentic match namespace. Focused unit tests cover drag move-id derivation, fallback exclusion, and hint copy; Playwright Chromium adds row drag/drop with invalid-drop no-op plus right-click no-preview, and selected-weather drag/drop to the Weather panel with card-flight and hidden-info assertions.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-04-cEp11.1-report.md` (cEp11.1). Backfills browser-level coverage for the cEp10 match-end ledger and cEp11 selected-card weather targeting without changing engine rules, legal moves, Redux adapter behavior, AI policy, product UI behavior, layout, styling, catalog data, route defaults, or hidden-info boundaries.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-04-cEp11-report.md` (cEp11). Polishes selected-card target presentation in `/?engine=1&ui=authentic` without changing engine rules or legal-move generation. Adds pure `buildSelectedCardTargetViewModel` helper in `src/components/gwent/matchViewModel.ts`; `AuthenticMatchScreen` no longer consumes `groupTargetActions` from the diagnostic engine shell helper for selected-card target presentation. Spatial board-row targets render as a dedicated `play here` overlay button on each legal row, with the row container demoted from `<button>` to `<div>` so board-card target buttons (still `<button>` for `card_instance` moves like Decoy) are never wrapped inside another button. Visible board-card targets render a `choose` badge plus accent ring on the card surface and dispatch the exact legal `PlayCard` move id; right-click inspection still works and never dispatches the move. Selecting Commander's Horn shows an explicit `horn slot` placeholder per legal own row inside the row's card strip. Selecting a weather card highlights the Weather panel with a `play weather` target button that dispatches the exact legal `weather` move whether the panel is empty or already populated. Global/no-target plays (e.g. Scorch) remain compact right-rail fallback buttons with safe lowercase labels and aria. The right rail's `Actions` block now consumes `SelectedCardTargetViewModel` directly: it shows a single hint line (`no card selected.` / `no legal targets.` / `choose a highlighted row.` / `choose a highlighted card.` / `choose a highlighted row or card.` / `choose a highlighted horn slot.` / `target the weather panel.` / `use the action button below.`) plus compact fallback action buttons only when the legal move requires them. All targets are reachable by keyboard with focus-visible styling using existing accent/ink/rule/parchment tokens — no one-off color system. Hidden-info safety: target labels never include raw `seat_a:...` / `seat_b:...` instance IDs or hidden opponent source IDs; cardLabel falls back to a public `card` literal when a card target is missing from the visible-card lookup. Adds 11 focused tests in `tests/components/gwent/authenticMatchViewModel.test.ts` (no-selection / no-targets / row / card / horn / weather / global fallback / hidden-info safety / missing-public-lookup fallback / recompute-on-card-change / mixed-target hint copy) plus two new Playwright Chromium smokes in `tests/e2e/engine-shell-smoke.spec.ts` (`authentic match highlights spatial board-row targets and dispatches the exact PlayCard move (cEp11)` and `authentic match avoids nested interactive elements on board rows (cEp11)`). Existing right-click inspection, leader status/prompt copy, round/match ledger, AI policy, simulation export, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, route promotion, and legacy UI behavior remain unchanged.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-04-cEp10-report.md` (cEp10). Polishes round resolution, match-end ledger, and post-match navigation in `/?engine=1&ui=authentic` without changing engine rules. Adds pure `buildMatchLedgerViewModel` and `buildResolveRoundActionViewModel` helpers in `src/components/gwent/matchViewModel.ts`; the round overlay component is now declarative and consumes a single ledger view model. Non-game-end ledgers render `You hold the field.` / `<opponent> holds the field.` / `Neither side yields.` titles with score, gem-loss `◆ before → after` rows (or `gem loss: none`), the engine-supplied next starter label, and one primary `next round →` action; dismissal only sets local state, never dispatches engine commands, never re-resolves rounds, and is keyed by `activeMatchKey` so rematch/setup/new match cannot reopen a stale dismissed overlay. Match-end ledgers render `Victory over <opponent>.` / `Defeat against <opponent>.` / `Draw.` titles with eyebrow `match concluded · X to Y`, real `roundHistory` rows, real standing rows (`result`, `rounds humanWins - aiWins`, `gems humanGems - aiGems` or `unknown` when unavailable), and route-aware navigation: `change deck` ghost + `rematch` primary when `onReturnToPreGame` is wired, otherwise `close` ghost + `rematch` primary. The right-rail action panel now consumes `buildResolveRoundActionViewModel`, which hides the action outside `round_end`, dispatches `resolveEngineRoundEnd(humanSeat)` exactly when enabled, and surfaces a single compact disabled reason inline (`prompt pending` / `waiting for opponent` / `waiting for round resolution`) without introducing a second action panel. Round ledger keeps `role="dialog"` / `aria-modal="true"` and ties its accessible name to the rendered ledger title; `Escape` dismisses non-game-end ledgers only. Click-outside is intentionally inert. No card source IDs, instance IDs, or hidden hand identities are surfaced in any ledger field. Adds 13 focused tests in `tests/components/gwent/authenticMatchViewModel.test.ts` covering both new helpers (round/match-end ledger shapes, draw/loss/gem-loss-fallback paths, navigation routing, no MMR/rank/streak/reward placeholders, no hidden-info leaks, `matchRunKey` invalidation; resolve-round visibility/enabled/disabled-reason matrix). No engine rule, legal-move generation, command execution, AI policy, simulation export, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, route promotion, or legacy UI behavior changed.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-04-cEp9-report.md` (cEp9). Makes the completed official leader system legible in `/?engine=1&ui=authentic` without changing engine rules. Adds pure `buildLeaderStatusViewModel` and `buildPromptPresentationViewModel` helpers in `src/components/gwent/matchViewModel.ts`; score cards now show compact active/passive/setup/ready/used/no-target/unavailable/current-round-cancelled leader status, with suppression derived from `seat.leaderCancelledRound === state.round` through the existing public selector flag. The action panel keeps dispatching exact legal `UseLeader` targets and now labels single active leaders with ability-specific copy such as `clear weather`, `restore card`, `discard and draw`, `look at hand`, and `cancel leader`, while disabled copy comes from the status view model rather than recomputed rules. Prompt panels now use ability-specific titles/copy for `cancel_leader`, `restore_discard_to_hand`, `draw_opponent_discard`, owner-visible `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck`, owner-visible `look_three_cards`, and `medic_revive`; prompt options are still sourced only from `choose_prompt_option` legal moves, with hidden owner-only prompts and non-owner prompt options redacted. Adds focused unit coverage plus browser assertions in the existing weather-choice and look-three-card smokes. No engine rule, legal-move generation, command execution, AI policy, simulation export, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, route promotion, or legacy UI behavior changed.

- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-04-cCp30-report.md` (cCp30). Adds post-leader regression coverage across the completed official leader matrix without adding new leader abilities. New test files: `tests/game/coreLeaderMatrixRegression.test.ts` (manifest/bucket invariants, active leader standard gate matrix for all 10 active categories, public event summary hidden-info safety) and `tests/game/coreLeaderCancelInteractions.test.ts` (White Flame proactive passive suppression outcomes, prompt coexistence/cancel/decline ordering, look-three-card prompt-local reveal boundaries, discard/draw deck prompt export redaction, Scorch under suppressed scoring policies, AI-owned reaction cancel, and safe simulation export validation from a suppressed leader state). Confirms public cCp29 counts remain `14 executable / 7 passive / 1 setup-time / 22 implemented / 0 placeholder`. Finds and fixes one localized production bug in `src/game/sim/exportObservation.ts`: cCp27 `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck` stage-2 own-deck prompt options now export prompt-local `own_deck_option_<index>` safe card refs to the acting perspective instead of targetless options; non-acting perspectives still receive `pendingPrompt: null`, and encoded legal actions still omit raw deck card instance IDs. No rule documentation changes were required; `docs/gwent-rules.md` and `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` remain current. Browser smoke was not run because cCp30 did not change product UI, routes, visible browser behavior, or Playwright coverage.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-04-cCp29-report.md` (cCp29). Implements `cancel_leader` for `nilfgaard.emhyr-var-emreis-the-white-flame` (Emhyr var Emreis: The White Flame) as the engine's first reaction / current-round suppression leader. New pure helper module `src/game/core/leaderCancel.ts` exporting `CANCEL_LEADER_SOURCE_ID`, `isLeaderSuppressedThisRound`, `getLeaderCancelStatus`, `canUseCancelLeaderProactively`, and `canOpenCancelLeaderReaction`. New `SeatState.leaderCancelledRound: number | null` field (initialized to `null` in `startMatch`, set to `state.round` on cancel, cleared at round transition). New `PendingPromptStage` value `"leader_cancel_reaction"` and new `PendingPromptContext.leaderCancel` field carrying the attempted leader's identity for the decline-replay path. New `GameEvent` variant `leader_cancelled { mode: "proactive" | "reaction", round, seatId, targetSeatId, targetLeaderCardId, targetLeaderSourceId, targetAbilityId }`. **Proactive flow:** `getLeaderMove` emits one no-target `use_leader` move on the White Flame seat's own turn against a cancellable opponent (active unused or implemented passive); setup-only `draw_extra_card` and already-used active leaders with no passive policy are excluded; cancel of cancel is forbidden. `executeLeader` sets opponent `leaderCancelledRound = state.round`, consumes White Flame, emits `ability_triggered → leader_cancelled (proactive) → ability_resolved.leader_cancelled → leader_used → turn_set`. The opponent leader is **not** consumed in proactive mode. **Reaction flow:** `executeLeader` adds a reaction-prompt gate before per-leader branches: when the opposing seat owns an unused, unsuppressed `cancel_leader`, the engine opens a `choose_option` prompt (`stage: "leader_cancel_reaction"`, `seatId: whiteFlameSeat`, two no-target options `cancel-leader:cancel` / `cancel-leader:decline`, `context.leaderCancel`) and returns without consuming any leader and without handing off the turn. Cancel option resolution: `prompt_resolved → ability_triggered → set target leaderCancelledRound → leader_cancelled (reaction) → ability_resolved.leader_cancelled → leader_used (White Flame) → leader_used (cancelled target) → turn_set` (handoff from attempted leader seat). Decline option resolution: `prompt_resolved → clear prompt → re-execute attempted leader exactly once with bypassCancelReaction: true and skipLegalCheck: true`. **Suppression:** every implemented active leader category is blocked by `getLeaderMove`' suppression check (clear weather, weather-pulling, row Scorch, optimize agile, restore discard, shuffle discards, draw opponent discard, discard/draw, look three cards). Manual `UseLeader` against a suppressed seat rejects through the existing legal-move assertion without mutating state. Passive scoring/ability helpers (`getWeatherPolicyBySeat`, `getRowHornPolicyBySeat`, `getDoubleSpiesPolicyBySeat`, `hasRandomMedicPolicyForSeat`) check `isLeaderSuppressedThisRound` and act as if the seat has no leader policy for the suppressed round. Setup-time `draw_extra_card` is not retroactively undone. `resolveRoundEnd` clears both seats' `leaderCancelledRound` to `null` before the next round starts. Promotes `cancel_leader` metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 13 to 14 (adds The White Flame); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 21 to 22; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 1 to 0 (removes `cancel_leader` — the list is now empty). AI: `legal-heuristic-v0`'s `choosePromptMove` deterministically picks the cancel option from a `cancel_leader` reaction prompt. Observation/export: extends `SeatObservation.ownLeader` / `opponentLeader` and `SafeSeatPrivatePublicState.leader` / `SafeOpponentPublicState.leader` with `cancelledThisRound: boolean`. UI: existing generic `PromptPanel` renders the two no-target reaction options without a new modal; `ScoreCard` shows `cancelled this round` when the leader is suppressed (clears at round transition); `summarizeEvents` renders `leader_cancelled` as a count-only label (`<actor> cancelled <target> leader for round N`). Adds 66 focused tests in `tests/game/coreCancelLeader.test.ts`. Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts`, `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts`, `tests/catalog/catalogValidators.test.ts`, `tests/components/gwent/cardStudioHelpers.test.ts`, `tests/components/gwent/authenticDeckBuilderViewModel.test.ts`, `tests/components/gwent/authenticMatchViewModel.test.ts`, `tests/game/coreDiscardDrawLeader.test.ts`, `tests/game/coreDrawExtraCardLeader.test.ts`, `tests/game/coreLookThreeCardsLeader.test.ts`, and `tests/game/coreRandomMedicLeader.test.ts` to reflect the new totals (14 executable + 7 passive + 1 setup = 22 implemented, 0 placeholder) and to drop the placeholder-leader fixtures (only `unknown_leader_ability` remains as a placeholder validator path). Living docs updated: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12n "Cancel Leader (cCp29)" documenting the reaction / current-round suppression contract, the proactive and reactive entry points, the cancel/decline event sequences, the round-transition clear, the cancel-of-cancel block, and the AI / UI / hidden-info safety; plus a §16 row for Emhyr: The White Flame; and updates §17.12d / §17.12e to point at §17.12n for the implemented suppression interaction. `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Emhyr: The White Flame's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp29)`, marks Pattern 6 IMPLEMENTED, marks Tranche 4 COMPLETE, refines Settled Product Decision §8 to mark it implemented, refreshes status counts (14 / 7 / 1 / 22 / 0), and appends a cCp29 change-log entry. No deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, asset, broad AI rewrite, route promotion, or legacy UI migration changes.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-04-cCp28-report.md` (cCp28). Implements `look_three_cards` for `nilfgaard.emhyr-var-emreis-emperor-of-nilfgaard` (Emhyr var Emreis: Emperor of Nilfgaard) as the engine's first hidden-info disclosure leader, opening Tranche 4 of `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md`. The leader emits one no-target `use_leader` move when the opponent hand has at least one card (`metadata.targetRequirement === "none"`, `metadata.targetCount === revealCount`, `metadata.targetLabel === "opponent hand"`, with diagnostic `opponentHandCount` / `revealCount`). New pure helper module `src/game/core/leaderLookThreeCards.ts` exporting `LOOK_THREE_CARDS_LEADER_SOURCE_ID`, `LOOK_THREE_CARDS_REVEAL_COUNT`, `getLookThreeCardsEligibility`, and `planLookThreeCardsReveal`. The helper selects up to three opponent hand cards via `createSeededRngFromState` + `shuffleWithRng`; RNG advances only when opponent hand length > 3 (length 1-3 reveals the full hand without invoking RNG and leaves `state.rng.state` unchanged). `executeLeader` advances RNG only when needed, emits `ability_triggered`, `opponent_hand_revealed { reason: "look_three_cards", seatId, opponentSeatId, cardIds, sourceIds }`, `ability_resolved.revealed_opponent_hand`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true` (the leader is consumed at `UseLeader`), emits `leader_used`, and opens an acknowledgement prompt (`kind === "choose_option"`, `stage === "opponent_hand_reveal"`, single option `look-three-cards:acknowledge` with `target.kind === "none"`, `context.revealedCardIds` carries the chosen opponent hand card IDs). `currentTurn` stays on the acting seat while the prompt is pending. Acknowledgement (`ChoosePromptOption { optionId: "look-three-cards:acknowledge" }`) emits `prompt_resolved`, clears `state.pendingPrompt`, and the outer command path hands off the turn through `handoffTurn`. After acknowledgement, no MatchState field retains the reveal — the snapshot lived only in `pendingPrompt.context.revealedCardIds`. Revealed cards are not moved out of the opponent's hand and do not trigger their abilities. Adds the new `GameEvent` variant `opponent_hand_revealed` (internal/raw event log only), the new `PendingPromptTarget` variant `{ kind: "none" }`, the new `PendingPromptStage` value `"opponent_hand_reveal"`, the new `PendingPromptContext.revealedCardIds` field, and new `UseLeaderMove.metadata` diagnostic fields `opponentHandCount` / `revealCount`. Hidden-info safety preserved: `getPromptMoves` returns `[]` to the non-acting seat; AI seat observation surfaces the revealed cards on `PendingPromptSummary.revealedCards` to the prompt owner only; safe simulation export exposes prompt-local refs `revealed_opponent_hand_<index>` to the acting perspective only; recent activity / event summaries render count-only labels ("Human looked at 3 opponent hand cards"). Promotes `look_three_cards` metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 12 to 13 (adds Emperor of Nilfgaard); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 20 to 21; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 2 to 1 (removes `look_three_cards`, leaving only `cancel_leader`). Adds 43 focused tests in `tests/game/coreLookThreeCardsLeader.test.ts` and 6 focused UI tests in `tests/components/gwent/authenticMatchViewModel.test.ts` for the new `buildLookThreeCardsRevealViewModel` helper; updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts`, `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts`, `tests/game/coreDiscardDrawLeader.test.ts`, `tests/game/coreDrawExtraCardLeader.test.ts`, `tests/game/coreRandomMedicLeader.test.ts`, `tests/components/gwent/authenticDeckBuilderViewModel.test.ts`, and `tests/components/gwent/authenticMatchViewModel.test.ts` to reflect the new totals (13 executable + 7 passive + 1 setup = 21 implemented, 1 placeholder) and to repoint placeholder-leader fixtures to `cancel_leader`. Living docs updated: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12m "Look Three Cards Leader (cCp28)" documenting the hidden-info disclosure contract, RNG policy, one-time modal contract, no-trigger contract for revealed cards, and hidden-info safety boundaries; plus a §16 row for Emperor of Nilfgaard; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Emperor of Nilfgaard's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp28)`, marks Pattern 5 IMPLEMENTED with one-time-modal details (replacing the older persistent-observation guidance), marks Tranche 4 with `look_three_cards` delivered, refines Settled Product Decisions §6 and §7 to mark them implemented, refreshes status counts, and appends a cCp28 change-log entry. UI ships a one-time `authentic-look-three-cards-dialog` modal for human-owned reveal prompts (with `authentic-look-three-cards-card` and `authentic-look-three-cards-ack` test IDs) and CSS hooks under the existing `authentic-match` namespace via the new `buildLookThreeCardsRevealViewModel` view-model helper; AI-owned reveal prompts continue to surface "AI resolving prompt from legal moves" without exposing card faces. The new selector `selectEngineLookThreeCardsReveal` exposes the revealed cards as engine view models only when the human seat owns the pending acknowledgement prompt. After cCp28 only `cancel_leader` (Pattern 6, current-round suppression) remains placeholder.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp27-report.md` (cCp27). Implements `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck` for `monsters.eredin-destroyer-of-worlds` (Eredin: Destroyer of Worlds) as the engine's first true active multi-stage prompt leader, completing Tranche 3 of `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md`. The leader requires at least one card in hand and one card in deck; `getLeaderMove` emits exactly one no-target `use_leader` move (`metadata.targetRequirement === "future_prompt"`, `metadata.targetLabel === "hand then deck"`, with diagnostic `discardMin: 1`, `discardMax: 2`, `handCount`, `deckCount`). `executeLeader` opens stage 1 (`pendingPrompt.kind === "choose_card_set"`, `stage === "discard_selection"`) with one option per legal 1-card discard followed by every 2-card combination in deterministic acting-seat hand order; stage 1 does **not** consume the leader. Stage 1 `ChoosePromptOption` validates 1-or-2 unique acting-hand cards, moves each selected card from hand to acting discard with `card_moved.reason === "leader_discard_for_draw"` (no battlefield-discard triggers — no Summon, Avenger, Medic, Spy, Scorch, weather, Muster, Berserker, Mardroeme), and immediately opens stage 2 (`pendingPrompt.kind === "choose_card"`, `stage === "deck_draw_selection"`, `context: { discardedCardIds }`) with one option per remaining acting-seat deck card. Stage 2 `ChoosePromptOption` validates the chosen card is still in the acting seat's deck, moves it to acting hand with `card_moved.reason === "leader_draw_from_deck"`, shuffles the remaining acting deck through `createSeededRngFromState` + `shuffleWithRng` (RNG advances only when remaining deck length ≥ 2; deck length 0 or 1 is a deterministic no-op), emits `deck_shuffled` with `reason === "leader_discard_draw"` (distinct from cCp23's `"leader_shuffle_into_deck"`), `prompt_resolved`, `ability_resolved.discarded_and_drew_card`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true`, emits `leader_used`, clears `state.pendingPrompt`, and hands off the turn. Drawn cards do **not** trigger their abilities. Hidden-info safety preserved: `getPromptMoves` returns `[]` for the non-acting seat at both stages; AI seat observations from `buildSeatObservation` return `pendingPrompt: null` to the wrong seat; simulation export observation surfaces the acting seat's own deck card to the acting perspective only via a prompt-local safe ref `own_deck_option_<index>` so raw deck instance IDs never appear in the safe export. Promotes `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck` metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`; updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 11 to 12 (adds Destroyer of Worlds); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 19 to 20; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 3 to 2 (removes `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck`). Adds 49 focused tests in `tests/game/coreDiscardDrawLeader.test.ts`; updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts`, `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts`, `tests/game/coreDrawExtraCardLeader.test.ts`, and `tests/game/coreRandomMedicLeader.test.ts` to reflect the new totals (12 executable + 7 passive + 1 setup = 20 implemented, 2 placeholder). Living docs updated: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12l and a §16 row for Eredin: Destroyer of Worlds; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Destroyer of Worlds to `implemented (cCp27)`, marks Pattern 3 IMPLEMENTED, marks Tranche 3 COMPLETE, refines Settled Product Decision §4 status, and appends a cCp27 change-log entry. Tranche 4 (`look_three_cards`, `cancel_leader`) is the only remaining tranche.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp26-report.md` (cCp26). Implements `draw_extra_card` for `scoiatael.francesca-findabair-daisy-of-the-valley` (Francesca Findabair: Daisy of the Valley) as a **setup-time initial hand-size modifier**, opening Tranche 3 of `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md`. Adds the new pure helper module `src/game/core/leaderSetup.ts` exporting `DRAW_EXTRA_CARD_LEADER_SOURCE_ID`, `BASE_INITIAL_HAND_SIZE`, and `getInitialHandDrawCountForLeader({ leaderSourceId, catalogLeaders, baseDrawCount? })`. The helper looks up the leader by source ID and returns `baseDrawCount + 1` (i.e. 11 by default) only when the resolved leader's `ability` is `draw_extra_card`; otherwise it returns the base count. Missing catalog entries, other abilities, or any inconsistent setup input fall back to the base count. `src/game/core/setup.ts` replaces the hard-coded `10` used for the initial draw with the helper-derived per-seat draw count, so the affected seat draws 11 initial cards (the next deterministic card off the seeded-RNG-shuffled deck top) instead of 10 before mulligan opens. Mulligan budget remains unchanged at two redraws; legal mulligan moves naturally include one-card mulligan options for all 11 hand cards (1 keep-hand + 11 one-card mulligans = 12 legal moves on the first tick). The leader emits no `use_leader` legal move, never sets `seat.leaderUsed`, and never emits `leader_used`; manual `UseLeader` rejects through the existing unsupported-leader path without state mutation. The 11th card is never chosen by prompt, kind, faction, strength, row, or ability — it is whatever the deterministic shuffle put on top. Tiny test decks shorter than 11 cards do not throw solely because of `draw_extra_card`; the existing `slice` behavior draws available cards. Setup events reuse existing types (`card_moved.reason === "initial_draw"` per drawn card and `initial_hand_drawn` with `cardIds.length === 11`); no new event types. Promotes `draw_extra_card` metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented` in `src/game/catalog/abilities.ts` with a description that captures the setup-time hand-size modifier contract. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: introduces a new bucket `implementedSetupLeaderSourceIds` (1 entry: `scoiatael.francesca-findabair-daisy-of-the-valley`) distinct from `executableLeaderSourceIds` (unchanged at 11) and `implementedPassiveLeaderSourceIds` (unchanged at 7); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` becomes the sorted union of all three (11 + 7 + 1 = 19); `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 4 to 3 (removes `draw_extra_card`). Adds 40 focused tests in `tests/game/coreDrawExtraCardLeader.test.ts` covering metadata promotion and manifest accounting (counts 11 / 7 / 1 / 19 / 3), helper outcomes (Daisy returns 11, non-Daisy returns 10, missing leader returns 10, custom `baseDrawCount` is honored, ability-not-source-ID gating), initial draw behavior (Daisy seat draws 11 / opposing non-Daisy still 10 / two-Daisy seats both 11 / `initial_hand_drawn` carries 11 IDs / 11 `card_moved.reason === "initial_draw"` events / hand vs deck zone placement / `leaderUsed` stays false / no `leader_used` / phase remains `mulligan` / `mulligansUsed` starts at 0), determinism (same-seed identical state, different seeds may differ, Daisy first-10 = non-Daisy first-10, Daisy 11th = non-Daisy deck top, no `Math.random` in `src/game/core`), legal moves (12 mulligan options on first tick, two-redraw cap unchanged, no `use_leader` move at any phase, manual `UseLeader` rejects without setting `leaderUsed`), regression (default Northern Realms vs Nilfgaard still draws 10/10, Daisy catalog entry still carries `draw_extra_card`, tiny <11-card test deck does not throw, no card lands in discard during setup, opponent hand IDs are not exposed through Daisy's setup events). Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 3 entries, implemented union grows to 19 with Daisy, new `implementedSetupLeaderSourceIds` assertion); `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts` (`unsupportedLeaderAbilityCounts.draw_extra_card` is undefined); `tests/game/coreRandomMedicLeader.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 3 entries, implemented union grows to 19, asserts setup-time bucket size 1). Updates living docs: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12k "Draw Extra Card Setup Leader (cCp26)" documenting the setup-time classification, draw-11 contract, top-of-shuffled-deck behavior, mulligan-budget invariance, no-`use_leader` / no-`leader_used` / no-`leaderUsed` contract, hidden-info safety, and the new manifest bucket; plus a §16 row for Francesca Findabair: Daisy of the Valley; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Daisy of the Valley's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp26, setup-time)`, replaces the predicted-behavior fields with the implemented contract, adds a new "Implemented setup-time leaders" table row, marks Pattern 7 IMPLEMENTED, marks Tranche 3 IN PROGRESS with `draw_extra_card` delivered and `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck` recommended next, refines the status counts (executable 11 unchanged, passive 7 unchanged, setup 1 new, placeholder 3), and appends a cCp26 change-log entry. No new helper export was avoided — the helper lives in the new `leaderSetup.ts` module per the spec recommendation. No UI route, layout, AI policy tuning, simulation engine, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp25-report.md` (cCp25). Implements `random_medic` for `nilfgaard.emhyr-var-emreis-invader-of-the-north` (Emhyr var Emreis: Invader of the North) as a **whole-match passive Medic mutation**, completing Tranche 2 of `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md`. Settles the cCp18 §C-3 conflict in favor of the Medic-mutation reading (settled product decision §5); the older "random Special replay from discard" wording is superseded. Adds the new pure helper module `src/game/core/leaderRandomMedic.ts` exporting `hasRandomMedicPolicyForSeat`, `getRandomMedicCandidates`, and `chooseRandomMedicCandidate`. While Invader of the North is the seat's leader, every non-hero Medic source controlled by that seat (`source.abilities.includes("medic") && source.kind !== "hero"`) revives a random eligible non-hero Unit from that seat's own discard pile (uniform over candidate cards through `createSeededRngFromState`; deterministic row fallback `close → ranged → siege` for multi-row targets) instead of opening a player-choice `medic_revive` prompt. Hero Medic sources are unaffected and continue to use the normal prompt path (Hero immunity, §17.1). Spy placement still flips to the opponent board side and resolves Spy draw; `controller` becomes the acting seat with `owner` preserved. The chain recurses for random-revived non-hero Medics until candidates are empty. RNG advances only on a real multi-candidate roll; zero or single candidate paths leave `state.rng.state` unchanged. Adds the new shared internal `placeMedicRevival` helper inside `src/game/core/abilities.ts` that the prompt-based and random-based Medic flows share, so Spy placement, controller reset, `card_played` emission, child ability resolution, and `settleMardroemeRow` cannot diverge. The new `ability_resolved` outcome `"random_revived_card"` distinguishes the random path from the prompt-based `"revived_card"`; no `prompt_opened` / `prompt_resolved` events are emitted for the random path. Promotes `random_medic` metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented` in `src/game/catalog/abilities.ts` with a description that captures the Medic-mutation contract. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `implementedPassiveLeaderSourceIds` grows from 6 to 7 (adds Invader of the North); `executableLeaderSourceIds` stays at 11 (passive, not executable); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 17 to 18; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 5 to 4 (removes `random_medic`). Adds 33 focused tests in `tests/game/coreRandomMedicLeader.test.ts` covering metadata promotion and manifest accounting (counts 11 / 7 / 18 / 4), helper outcomes (empty / single / kind filters / opponent-discard exclusion / side-deck-only generated / off-owner / stale entries / multi-row single-candidate / discard order), legal-move passive contract (no `use_leader` move emitted, manual `UseLeader` rejects without leader consumption, The Relentless `draw_opponent_discard` unaffected), random resolution (no prompt events, deterministic single-candidate revive without RNG advance, deterministic multi-candidate pick with RNG advance, Spy placement / draw / off-owner owner preservation, Muster chain after revival, Mardroeme transform of revived Berserker), Medic chaining (chain recurses through random policy, ends cleanly with `no_targets` when discard empties), and regression guards (non-Invader leaders still open the prompt, hero Medic source under Invader still opens the prompt via a test-local hero Medic fixture, cCp22 restore-to-hand and cCp24 draw-opponent-discard prompts still return cards to hand only and do not trigger Medic). Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 4 entries, passive set grows to 7 with Invader, implemented set grows to 18, sanity assertion that `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` does not contain `random_medic`). Updates living docs: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12j "Random Medic Passive Leader (cCp25)" documenting the passive contract, non-hero Medic source scope, hero Medic source exception, own-discard non-hero unit candidate filter, random selection / RNG advancement, deterministic row fallback, Spy placement / draw, Medic chain behavior, and the no-`use_leader` / no-`leader_used` / no-`leaderUsed` contract, plus a §16 row for Invader of the North; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Invader of the North's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp25, passive)`, replaces the stale "random Special replay" body text with the settled Medic-mutation contract, renames Pattern 8 from "Random Special Replay From Discard" to "Passive Random Medic Mutation" and marks it IMPLEMENTED, marks Tranche 2 COMPLETE, marks §C-3 SETTLED & IMPLEMENTED while preserving historical conflict context, refines the Engine and UI Implications row 8, and appends a cCp25 change-log entry. No UI route, layout, AI policy tuning, simulation engine, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp24-report.md` (cCp24). Implements `draw_opponent_discard` on `nilfgaard.emhyr-var-emreis-the-relentless` (Emhyr var Emreis: The Relentless) as an active one-shot leader resolved through a single-step `choose_card` prompt over the **opponent's** discard pile. Settles the cCp18 §C-2 conflict in favor of the catalog (catalog wins; the printed-rulebook deck-tutor wording is superseded). Adds the pure helper `getOpponentDiscardDrawCandidates({ state, seatId, catalogCards })` in `src/game/core/leaderOpponentDiscardDraw.ts` that returns the **opponent's** discard cards in opponent discard order, skipping missing instances and missing catalog sources. Any card kind in opponent discard is eligible — units, heroes, specials, weather, side-deck-only / generated, and off-owner cards (acting-seat-owned cards physically sitting in opponent discard). `getLeaderMove` emits exactly one no-target `use_leader` move (`target.kind === "none"`, `metadata.targetRequirement === "future_prompt"`, `metadata.targetCount === candidates.length`, `metadata.targetLabel === "opponent discard"`) when the opponent discard is non-empty; empty opponent discard emits no legal move (even if own discard has cards). `executeLeader` opens a `pendingPrompt` (`kind === "choose_card"`, `abilityId === "draw_opponent_discard"`, one option per candidate keyed `draw-opponent-discard:<cardId>` with label `"Draw <card name> from opponent discard"`, `target.kind === "card_instance"` without `row`) **without** consuming the leader; `currentTurn` stays on the acting seat while the prompt is pending. `getPromptMoves` now selects target side by `prompt.abilityId`: `side: "opponent"` with `seatId: opponentSeat` for `draw_opponent_discard`, `side: "own"` with `seatId: actingSeat` for cCp22 restore prompts (preserved). Medic row-bearing targets unchanged. `ChoosePromptOption` validates the chosen card is still in the opponent's discard pile (rejects with `EngineRuleError` if not — leader untouched), moves it from opponent discard to acting hand with the new `card_moved.reason === "leader_draw_opponent_discard_to_hand"`, sets the chosen card's `controller` to the acting seat (leaving `owner` unchanged), emits `prompt_resolved`, `ability_resolved.drew_opponent_discard`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true`, emits `leader_used`, clears the pending prompt, and hands off the turn through `handoffTurn`. Drawn cards do **not** resolve their abilities — they enter hand and behave normally only if played later. Off-owner cards in opponent discard are eligible; the chosen card moves to acting hand with `owner` preserved and `controller` reset to acting. Promotes `draw_opponent_discard` metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 10 to 11 (adds The Relentless); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 16 to 17; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 6 to 5 (removes `draw_opponent_discard`). Adds 31 focused tests in `tests/game/coreDrawOpponentDiscardLeader.test.ts` covering metadata promotion, manifest accounting, helper outcomes (empty / opponent-vs-own / opponent discard order / mixed kinds / side-deck-only generated / missing instance / missing source / determinism), legal-move integration (no-move on empty opponent / no-move when only own discard has cards / one move on non-empty / metadata shape / `targetCount` scaling / no move post-use), command execution (non-`none` target rejection / empty-opponent-discard rejection / prompt-open with no-card-moved / no leaderUsed flip / current turn unchanged / opponent discard not yet emptied), prompt resolution (full event sequence with controller change and owner preservation / off-owner card draw / unit-hero-special-weather choice / no Medic / Scorch / weather trigger), rejection paths (wrong seat / invalid option / stale target no longer in opponent discard / cannot choose own-discard card), prompt move shape (opponent-side `card_instance` target / hidden info for non-acting seat / cCp22 restore own-side target shape regression), seatObservation hidden-info safety, and the post-use no-leader-move contract. Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 5 entries, executable set grows to 11 with The Relentless, implemented set grows to 17) and `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts` (`unsupportedLeaderAbilityCounts.draw_opponent_discard` is undefined). Updates `tests/catalog/catalogValidators.test.ts` (placeholder leader assertion repointed from `draw_opponent_discard` to `cancel_leader`). Updates `tests/components/gwent/authenticDeckBuilderViewModel.test.ts` (placeholder-leader fixtures repointed to `look_three_cards` since The Relentless is now implemented; the current Nilfgaard preset itself uses The Relentless and is now warning-free, so the placeholder-warning test overrides the leader). Updates living docs: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12i documenting the active-one-shot contract over opponent discard and updates the §16 Emhyr: The Relentless row to the cCp24 settled contract; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips The Relentless's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp24)`, replaces the conflict-pending wording with the settled catalog-wins contract, adds an active-executable table row, marks Pattern 4 IMPLEMENTED for both members (cCp22 and cCp24), updates Tranche 2 ordering, refines the Engine and UI Implications row 4, marks §C-2 SETTLED & IMPLEMENTED, and appends a cCp24 change-log entry. No UI route, layout, AI policy tuning, simulation engine logic, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp23-report.md` (cCp23). Implements `shuffle_discards_into_decks` for `skellige.crach-an-craite` (Crach an Craite) as an active one-shot leader. Adds the pure helper `getDiscardRecyclePlan({ state })` in `src/game/core/leaderDiscardRecycle.ts` that returns the deterministic seat-ordered recycle plan (`seat_a` then `seat_b`), preserving discard-insertion order inside each seat plan and skipping stale missing-card entries. `getLeaderMove` emits exactly one no-target `use_leader` move (`target.kind === "none"`, `metadata.targetRequirement === "none"`, `metadata.targetCount === plan.totalCardCount`, `metadata.targetLabel === "discard piles"`) when at least one discard pile is non-empty; both-empty discards emit no legal move (per settled product decision §10). `executeLeader` rejects any non-`none` target, rejects an empty plan, then clones state, emits `ability_triggered`, processes affected seats in stable order through a single `SeededRng` instance built from `state.rng.seed` / `state.rng.state`. Per affected seat: every planned card is moved from discard to that seat's deck with `card_moved.reason === "leader_shuffle_into_deck"`, the moved card's `controller` is reset to the destination deck seat (with `owner` unchanged), then `shuffleWithRng` shuffles the seat's full deck and `deck_shuffled.reason === "leader_shuffle_into_deck"` is emitted. Empty-discard seats are not shuffled (no hidden no-op mutation) and emit no `deck_shuffled`. After all affected shuffles, `state.rng.state` is updated to the RNG's final state. The transaction emits `ability_resolved` with `outcome === "shuffled_discards"`, `leader_used`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true`, and hands off the turn through `handoffTurn`. Recycled cards do **not** resolve their abilities (no `card_played`, no Medic chain, no Scorch, no on-play weather effect) — they return to deck and behave normally only if drawn and played later. Off-owner discard cards (Spies discarded after round cleanup on the opposite board side) recycle into the discard-pile seat's deck, not the original owner's deck — `controller` is reset to the destination seat, `owner` is preserved. Removed-from-game, side deck, hand, board, row horns, weather zone, and leader zone are not touched. Promotes `shuffle_discards_into_decks` metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 9 to 10 (adds Crach an Craite); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 15 to 16; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 7 to 6 (removes `shuffle_discards_into_decks`). Adds 26 focused tests in `tests/game/coreShuffleDiscardsLeader.test.ts` covering metadata promotion, manifest accounting, helper outcomes (empty plan / stable seat order / single non-empty seat / stale missing-instance entry / determinism), legal-move integration (no-move on both-empty / one-move on acting-only-discard / one-move on opp-only-discard / one-move on both-non-empty / metadata shape / no-move post-use), command execution (non-none target rejection / empty-discard rejection / full transaction with both seats / off-owner card handling / untouched-zone preservation / event-sequence assertion / affected-decks-only / no-trigger-on-restore), RNG determinism (state advances / identical seeds yield identical post-state / different seeds yield different orders / unaffected-seat deck unchanged), and the post-use no-leader-move contract. Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 6 entries, executable list grows to 10 with Crach an Craite, implemented set grows to 16) and `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts` (`unsupportedLeaderAbilityCounts.shuffle_discards_into_decks` is undefined). Updates `tests/components/gwent/authenticMatchViewModel.test.ts` (the placeholder-status fixture switched from Crach to Emhyr var Emreis: Emperor of Nilfgaard with `look_three_cards`). Updates living docs: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12h documenting the active-one-shot contract and the §16 Crach an Craite FAQ row; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Crach an Craite's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp23)`, replaces the predicted behavior fields with the implemented contract, adds an active-executable table row, marks Pattern 10 IMPLEMENTED, updates Tranche 2 ordering, refines the Engine and UI Implications row 10, and appends a cCp23 change-log entry. No UI route, layout, AI policy, simulation engine logic, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-02-cCp22-report.md` (cCp22). Implements `restore_discard_to_hand` on `monsters.eredin-bringer-of-death` (Eredin: Bringer of Death) as an active one-shot leader with a single-step `choose_card` prompt over the acting seat's own discard pile. Adds the pure helper `getRestoreDiscardCandidates({ state, seatId, catalogCards })` in `src/game/core/leaderDiscardRestore.ts` that returns the acting seat's own-discard cards in discard order, skipping missing instances and missing catalog sources. Any card kind in own discard is eligible — units, heroes, specials, weather, and side-deck-only / generated cards. `getLeaderMove` emits exactly one no-target `use_leader` move (`targetRequirement === "future_prompt"`, `targetCount === candidates.length`, `targetLabel === "own discard"`) when the discard is non-empty; empty discard emits no legal move. `executeLeader` opens a `pendingPrompt` (`kind === "choose_card"`, `abilityId === "restore_discard_to_hand"`, one option per candidate keyed `restore:<cardId>` with label `"Restore <card name> to hand"`, `target.kind === "card_instance"` without `row`) **without** consuming the leader; `currentTurn` stays on the acting seat while the prompt is pending. `ChoosePromptOption` validates the chosen card is still in the acting seat's discard pile (rejects with `EngineRuleError` if not — leader untouched), moves it from discard to hand with the new `card_moved.reason === "leader_restore_discard_to_hand"`, sets the restored card's `controller` to the acting seat (leaving `owner` unchanged), emits `prompt_resolved`, `ability_resolved.restored_card`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true`, emits `leader_used`, clears the pending prompt, and hands off the turn through the existing `handoffTurn` helper. Restored cards do **not** resolve their abilities (no `card_played`, no Medic chain, no Scorch, no on-play weather effect) — they return to hand and behave normally only if played later. `PendingPromptOption.target.row` is now optional; Medic continues to set `row`, the new restore prompt omits it. Promotes `restore_discard_to_hand` metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 8 to 9 (adds Bringer of Death); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 14 to 15; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 8 to 7 (removes `restore_discard_to_hand`). Adds 31 focused tests in `tests/game/coreRestoreDiscardLeader.test.ts` covering metadata promotion, manifest accounting, helper outcomes (empty / discard-order / mixed kinds / side-deck-only generated / opponent-discard exclusion / missing instance / missing source / determinism), legal-move integration (no-move on empty / one move on non-empty / targetCount scaling / no move post-use / own-only contract), command execution (prompt structure & seat ownership / event sequence at prompt-open / no card moved / leaderUsed unchanged / currentTurn unchanged / non-none target rejection / empty-discard rejection), prompt resolution (full event sequence including controller change with owner preserved / Yennefer Medic-on-restore non-trigger / hero eligibility / special+weather eligibility), rejection (wrong seat / invalid option ID / stale target / missing prompt / no leader move post-restore), legal-prompt-moves shape (no-row card_instance targets / hidden info for non-acting seat), and a Medic regression to ensure Medic prompts still get row-bearing card_instance targets. Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 7 entries, executable list grows to 9 with Bringer of Death, implemented set grows to 15) and `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts` (`unsupportedLeaderAbilityCounts.restore_discard_to_hand` is undefined). Updates living docs: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12g documenting the active-one-shot contract, eligibility, prompt-open transaction, prompt-resolution transaction, restored-card-no-trigger contract, and the AI/UI hidden-info safety; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Eredin: Bringer of Death's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp22)`, adds an active-executable table row, marks Pattern 4 IMPLEMENTED, updates Tranche 2 ordering, refines the Engine and UI Implications row 4 (now reflecting `targetCount` / `targetLabel` metadata), and appends a cCp22 change-log entry. No UI route, layout, AI policy, simulation engine logic, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.

- Earlier reference: `audit/reports/2026-05-02-cCp21-report.md` (cCp21). Implements `optimize_agile_rows` on `scoiatael.francesca-findabair-hope-of-the-aen-seidhe` as an active one-shot leader. Adds the pure planning helper `planOptimizeAgileRows({ state, seatId, catalogCards, catalogLeaders })` in `src/game/core/leaderOptimizeAgile.ts` that collects own-board non-hero Agile units, intersects their legal rows for the common destination, simulates each candidate row through `calculateScores`, scores all candidate rows before filtering for executable movement, and returns `{ outcome, currentScore, bestScore?, eligibleCards, candidates, bestCandidates }`. Pure no-op rows are never executable; worse movable rows are also not offered when the current no-op row is uniquely best. `getLeaderMove` calls the helper and emits no `use_leader` move on `no_eligible` / `no_candidate_row` / `no_executable_row`; emits a single no-target `use_leader` (`targetRequirement === "none"`) on `auto`; emits one `board_row` move per tied best row (`targetRequirement === "agile_row_choice"`) on `choice`. `metadata.targetLabel` is set to `"Close Combat"` / `"Ranged Combat"` / `"Siege Combat"` so the existing cEp8 leader-choice menu renders without UI changes. `executeLeader` recomputes the plan, validates the target shape (no-target for auto; tied best acting-seat board-row for choice), moves all eligible cards in deterministic board order (`close → ranged → siege`, preserving unit order), emits `card_moved.reason === "leader_optimize_agile"` per moved card (cards already on the chosen row do not move and produce no event), emits `ability_triggered` / `ability_resolved.moved` / `leader_used`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true`, and hands off the turn. Promotes `optimize_agile_rows` metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`; adds the new `card_moved.reason "leader_optimize_agile"` to the engine event union; extends the `UseLeaderMove.metadata` `targetRequirement` union with `"agile_row_choice"` and adds optional `currentScore` / `bestScore` / `candidateRows` fields. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 7 to 8 (adds Hope of the Aen Seidhe); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 13 to 14; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 9 to 8 (removes `optimize_agile_rows`). Adds 31 focused tests in `tests/game/coreOptimizeAgileRows.test.ts` covering metadata promotion, manifest accounting, helper outcomes (no_eligible / hero exclusion / opponent-side exclusion / agile-spy fixture exclusion), unique-best-from-scoring, weather-flees / horn-attracts / morale-attracts row selection, tied-row choice, no-op exclusion, worse-row rejection when current row is uniquely best, determinism, legal-move integration (auto, choice, no-eligible, current-best no-emit, post-use), command execution (auto with three-card move, skip-already-on-row, choice rejecting non-best / wrong-seat / no-target / extra-target), no-mutation on rejection, manual-UseLeader-after-use returns no leader move, and the standard turn / pass / phase / prompt gates. Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 8, executable list grows to 8 with Hope of the Aen Seidhe, implemented set grows to 14); updates `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts` (`unsupportedLeaderAbilityCounts.optimize_agile_rows` is undefined). Updates living docs: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12f documenting the active-one-shot contract, eligibility, candidate-row selection, auto-vs-tied-choice resolution, mutation/event contract, and the cEp8 menu compatibility note; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips the Hope of the Aen Seidhe metadata status to `implemented (cCp21)`, replaces stale "row of your choice" prose with the settled auto/tie-choice contract, marks Pattern 9 IMPLEMENTED, refines Settled Product Decision §3, updates Tranche 2 ordering, updates the Engine and UI Implications row 9, and appends a cCp21 change-log entry. No UI route, layout, AI policy, simulation engine logic, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- Earlier reference: `cCp20` report at `audit/reports/2026-05-02-cCp20-report.md` (cCp20). Implements `double_spies` on `monsters.eredin-breacc-glas-the-treacherous` as a **whole-match passive** scoring policy, per the post-cCp18 product decision. Adds the pure helper `getDoubleSpiesPolicyBySeat(state, leaders)` in `src/game/core/scoring.ts` mirroring `getWeatherPolicyBySeat` (cCp15) and `getRowHornPolicyBySeat` (cCp19); threads it through `calculateScores` with an explicit `doubleSpiesPolicyBySeat` override; adds `CardScoreEntry.spyMultiplier` / `afterSpyMultiplier` fields and a distinct `"leader_double_spies"` modifier marker. Modifier order is now Weather → Double Spies → Tight Bond → Morale → Horn (per the spec). Promotes `double_spies` metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`, adds `monsters.eredin-breacc-glas-the-treacherous` to `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest.implementedPassiveLeaderSourceIds` (now 6 entries: King Bran + four cCp19 row-horn + cCp20 Treacherous), regenerates `implementedLeaderSourceIds` (13 entries), and removes `double_spies` from `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` (now 9 entries). Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 9, implemented set grows to 13, passive set grows to 6); `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts` (`unsupportedLeaderAbilityCounts.double_spies` is undefined). Updates `AuthenticMatchScreen.tsx` to consume the helper, render a compact `authentic-leader-choice-menu` popover near the existing leader action when more than one legal move is available (trigger label `choose weather`, `aria-haspopup="menu"`, `aria-expanded` reflecting open state, `aria-controls="authentic-leader-choice-menu"` while open), keep single/no-target leaders as a direct `use leader` action, dispatch the exact `UseLeader` command for the selected option's `move.target`, and close the menu on selection, click-outside (window `mousedown` listener), Escape, leader-disabled, or stale legal-move recompute (kind no longer `"choice"`). Required test IDs land: `authentic-leader-action`, `authentic-leader-choice-menu`, `authentic-leader-choice-option`. No engine rule, legal-move generation, command execution, AI policy, simulation engine, deck builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes. - Earlier reference: `cCp17` report at `audit/reports/2026-05-01-cCp17-report.md`. Promotes generic `summon` ability metadata from `planned` to `implemented`, distinguishing it from `avenger` so Summon triggers only when a board-row source is **discarded** (Special Scorch, unit row-Scorch, unit-source whole-board Scorch, Foltest `scorch_range`/`scorch_siege` leaders, round cleanup) while Avenger keeps its broader battlefield-removal contract (round cleanup, Scorch, Decoy bounce). Adds a centralised pure helper `resolveSummonForCard` in `src/game/core/abilities.ts` that mirrors `resolveAvengerForCard` shape, returns `"no_summon" | "missing_link" | "missing_replacement" | "missing_origin_row" | "summoned"`, looks up `linkedSourceIds[0]` from the **controlling seat's** side deck, lands the replacement on the source's previous board side and row, sets the replacement's controller to the source's controller, and emits structured `ability_resolved` outcomes for the missing-link / missing-replacement / missing-origin-row paths plus a successful `card_summoned { abilityId: "summon" }` and `ability_resolved.summoned`. Widens `card_moved.reason` with a new `"summon_replacement"` movement reason (distinct from Avenger's `"avenger_summon"`) and `card_summoned.abilityId` from `"avenger"` to `"avenger" | "summon"` in `src/game/core/types.ts`; the existing Avenger event shape stays unchanged. Replaces the old `ability_deferred { abilityId: "summon", reason: "planned_ability" }` on-play emit with `ability_triggered` + `ability_resolved.armed_for_discard` to match Avenger's "armed" style without summoning anything on play. Wires the resolver into `resolveScorchRow` and `resolveUnitScorchGlobal` (`src/game/core/abilities.ts`), `resolveSpecialScorch` and the `executeLeader` row-Scorch leader branch (`src/game/core/commands.ts`), and the round-cleanup `sweepBattlefield` helper (after the existing `board_swept` event so replacements persist into the next round; collected before discard, resolved after the sweep, parallel to the existing Avenger collection). Drops the `summon` half of `emitDiscardTriggerDeferrals` in both `src/game/core/abilities.ts` (now a comment-only no-op for Summon — the helper continues to emit Avenger deferrals through the call sites in `commands.ts`) and `src/game/core/commands.ts` so no `ability_deferred { abilityId: "summon" }` events fire under normal play; existing `avenger` deferrals stay intact for event-trace stability. Decoy bounce, Medic revival, Skellige round-three return, and Monsters keep all remain non-Summon paths because they are not discards. Replacements are summoned, not "played" — the engine does not call `resolveCardAbilities` on the replacement; ongoing scoring effects apply naturally once the replacement is on the board. Catalog promotion counts are unchanged in cCp17. Official porting drops `unsupportedAbilityCounts.summon` automatically because the Game8 staging contains no `summon` candidates and the metadata is now `implemented`. Adds `docs/gwent-rules.md` §17.10 Summon Interactions update documenting the discard-from-board-only trigger, controller-side-deck replacement, board-side placement, missing-link/replacement/origin outcomes, the Summon-vs-Avenger distinction, and the explicit non-trigger list (Decoy, Medic, Skellige round-three return, Monsters keep, weather cleanup). Adds 21 focused tests in a new `tests/game/coreSummonDiscardTrigger.test.ts` covering metadata, helper outcomes (no_summon, missing_link, missing_replacement, missing_origin_row, summoned), on-play armed_for_discard, non-trigger cases (Decoy, Medic, Skellige round-three return, Monsters keep), trigger paths (Special Scorch, unit row-Scorch, unit whole-board Scorch, Foltest row-Scorch leader, round cleanup, board-side discard preservation), Avenger interaction (Decoy + Avenger still summons, Avenger Scorch still summons, Avenger round cleanup still summons), and a wrong-target rejection. The cCp13 `it.todo` for Summon in `tests/catalog/linkedAbilityInvariants.test.ts` is promoted to an active metadata-status invariant under a new `Summon discard-trigger lock (cCp17)` block; the active engine regressions live in the new focused test file. Updates `tests/catalog/catalogValidators.test.ts` to assert `CATALOG_ABILITY_METADATA.summon.status === "implemented"` (was `planned`). One `it.todo` remains, scoped to placeholder leader tutor/restore effects (Eredin, Emhyr, etc.). No UI route, layout, AI policy, headless simulation engine logic, deck-builder layout, Card Studio layout, image asset, JSONL boundary, simulation export schema, or Playwright smoke test changes. Catalog promotion is intentionally untouched — Cow and Kambi remain `avenger`, Bovine Defense Force remains ability-less, and the combined Cow/Bovine staging candidate stays blocked by its catalog-split/Avenger issues.

It keeps all legality and command mutation engine-owned, dispatches exact existing `PlayCard` legal moves, and avoids nested interactive board rows.

## Required Reading For Every Coding Instance

- `AGENTS.md`
- `docs/PROJECT_STATE.md`
- Active spec under `docs/spec/` when one is assigned
- Latest relevant report under `audit/reports/`
- `docs/gwent-rules.md` when touching rules
- For Cluster E UI work: `docs/overhaul-plan/cluster-e-ui-plan.md`, `docs/ui-handoff/README.md`, and the relevant prototype file under `docs/ui-handoff/prototype/`

## Current Architecture

- `/` now opens the authentic product pre-game setup screen.
- `/legacy` remains the temporary legacy Redux UI holding route.
- `/engine-diagnostic` opens the diagnostic engine shell. The old `/?engine=1` route remains a compatibility alias; `VITE_ENGINE_UI=1` no longer makes `/` leave the authentic pre-game route.
- Canonical authentic routes are `/match`, `/deck-builder`, `/card-studio`, `/official-porting`, `/components`, `/ui-harness`, and `/ai-lab`. Old `/?engine=1&ui=authentic&view=...` URLs remain compatibility aliases.
- The pure engine under `src/game/core/` owns rules, legal moves, scoring, prompts, command transactions, round resolution, and game end.
- The Redux engine adapter stores `MatchState`, selected product `aiPolicyId`, runtime catalog snapshot, status/locks, command history, event logs, adapter errors, and UI-only selection while dispatching engine commands.
- Catalog and deck preset data under `src/game/catalog/` and `src/data/catalog/` define card, leader, faction, ability metadata, and current playable presets.
- Authentic UI components under `src/components/gwent/` provide the production card visual path, faction/row/ability/leader display metadata, and the `/ui-harness` review route. They do not import legacy rule helpers, legacy AI modules, or engine board state, and they are covered by the forbidden-imports check.
- Quarantined legacy gameplay source lives under `src/legacy/`; new product UI, engine UI, pure engine, simulation, benchmark, catalog, and AI-policy code must not import `@/legacy/...`.
- The selected front-facing product UI direction is the `docs/ui-handoff/` Authentic Tactical Card Table handoff. The diagnostic engine shell remains available at `/engine-diagnostic`, while `/` is now the authentic product route.
