| `cCp29` | Cluster C Phase 29 | `audit/reports/2026-05-04-cCp29-report.md` | Cancel Leader Current-Round Suppression (Tranche 4 closes; the official leader matrix is complete). Promotes `cancel_leader` leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`, making `nilfgaard.emhyr-var-emreis-the-white-flame` (Emhyr var Emreis: The White Flame) mechanically active as the engine's first reaction / current-round suppression leader. Adds the new pure helper module `src/game/core/leaderCancel.ts` exporting `CANCEL_LEADER_SOURCE_ID`, `isLeaderSuppressedThisRound`, `getLeaderCancelStatus`, `canUseCancelLeaderProactively`, and `canOpenCancelLeaderReaction`. Adds the new `SeatState.leaderCancelledRound: number | null` field (initialized to `null` in `startMatch`, set to `state.round` on cancel, cleared at round transition by `resolveRoundEnd`). Adds the new `PendingPromptStage` value `"leader_cancel_reaction"`, the new `PendingPromptContext.leaderCancel: LeaderCancelReactionContext` field carrying the attempted leader's identity (and the original `target`, `unknown`-typed to avoid a cycle with `LegalMoveTarget`) for the decline-replay path, and the new `GameEvent` variant `leader_cancelled { mode: "proactive" \| "reaction", round, seatId, targetSeatId, targetLeaderCardId, targetLeaderSourceId, targetAbilityId }`. **Proactive flow:** `getLeaderMove` emits one no-target `use_leader` move on the White Flame seat's own normal turn when the opponent has a cancellable current-round leader effect (active unused or implemented passive). Setup-only `draw_extra_card` and already-used active leaders with no passive policy are excluded; cancel of cancel is forbidden. `executeLeader` rejects non-`none` targets, sets `state.seats[opponent].leaderCancelledRound = state.round`, consumes White Flame, and emits `ability_triggered → leader_cancelled (proactive) → ability_resolved.leader_cancelled → leader_used → turn_set`. The opponent leader is **not** consumed in proactive mode. **Reaction flow:** `executeLeader` adds a reaction-prompt gate before per-leader branches: when a non-`cancel_leader` active leader fires and the opposing seat owns an unused, unsuppressed `cancel_leader` and has not passed and is not itself suppressed, the engine clones state, opens `pendingPrompt` (`choose_option`, `stage: "leader_cancel_reaction"`, `seatId: whiteFlameSeat`, two no-target options `cancel-leader:cancel` / `cancel-leader:decline`, `context.leaderCancel`), emits `prompt_opened`, and returns without consuming any leader and without handing off the turn. Cancel option resolution (in `commands.choosePromptOption`): emits `prompt_resolved → ability_triggered → set target leaderCancelledRound → leader_cancelled (reaction) → ability_resolved.leader_cancelled → leader_used (White Flame) → leader_used (cancelled target) → turn_set` (handoff from attempted leader seat). Decline option resolution: emits `prompt_resolved → clear prompt → re-execute attempted leader exactly once with bypassCancelReaction: true and skipLegalCheck: true` so the attempted leader fires through its existing per-leader branch without re-opening the same reaction prompt. **Suppression:** every implemented active leader category is blocked by `getLeaderMove`'s suppression check (clear weather, weather-pulling, row Scorch, optimize agile, restore discard, shuffle discards, draw opponent discard, discard/draw, look three cards). Manual `UseLeader` against a suppressed seat rejects through the existing legal-move assertion without mutating state. Passive scoring/ability helpers (`getWeatherPolicyBySeat`, `getRowHornPolicyBySeat`, `getDoubleSpiesPolicyBySeat`, `hasRandomMedicPolicyForSeat`) check `isLeaderSuppressedThisRound` and act as if the seat has no leader policy for the suppressed round. Setup-time `draw_extra_card` is not retroactively undone — the extra card already entered hand at `startMatch` and there is no current-round policy to suppress. `resolveRoundEnd` clears both seats' `leaderCancelledRound` to `null` before the next round starts, so passive helpers resume normally and active leaders suppressed proactively (but not consumed) become legal again. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 13 to 14 (adds The White Flame); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 21 to 22; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 1 to 0 — the list is now empty. AI: extends `legal-heuristic-v0`'s `choosePromptMove` so the AI deterministically picks the cancel option when it owns a `cancel_leader` reaction prompt. Observation/export: extends `SeatObservation.ownLeader` / `opponentLeader` and `SafeSeatPrivatePublicState.leader` / `SafeOpponentPublicState.leader` with `cancelledThisRound: boolean`. UI: existing generic `PromptPanel` renders the two no-target reaction options without a new modal; `ScoreCard` shows `cancelled this round` when `seat.leaderCancelledRound === match.round` (clears at round transition); `summarizeEvents` renders `leader_cancelled` as a count-only public label (`<actor> cancelled <target> leader for round N`). Adds 66 focused tests in `tests/game/coreCancelLeader.test.ts` covering metadata / manifest, helpers (`isLeaderSuppressedThisRound`, `getLeaderCancelStatus`, `canUseCancelLeaderProactively`, `canOpenCancelLeaderReaction`), proactive legal moves and command (gating, target rejection, opponent not consumed, event order, blocks active getLeaderMove for current round), round transition (clears suppression, suppressed unused active leader becomes legal again next round), passive scoring/ability suppression (King Bran, row horns, Double Spies, Random Medic; setup-time `draw_extra_card` not retroactively undone; `calculateScores` recomputes without King Bran modifier when suppressed), legal-move guard for every implemented active leader category while suppressed, manual `UseLeader` rejection on a suppressed seat, reaction prompt open / cancel option (consumes both leaders, blocks weather-pulling effect, hands off from attempted leader seat, event sequence with both `leader_used` events, look-three-cards reaction cancel does not emit `opponent_hand_revealed`, second use of either consumed leader is illegal), reaction decline option (no consume, resolves original leader exactly once, does not reopen the prompt), AI policy choosing cancel deterministically, seat observation / safe simulation export `cancelledThisRound` flag, and product UI prompt-shape regression. Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts`, `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts`, `tests/catalog/catalogValidators.test.ts`, `tests/components/gwent/cardStudioHelpers.test.ts`, `tests/components/gwent/authenticDeckBuilderViewModel.test.ts`, `tests/components/gwent/authenticMatchViewModel.test.ts`, `tests/game/coreDiscardDrawLeader.test.ts`, `tests/game/coreDrawExtraCardLeader.test.ts`, `tests/game/coreLookThreeCardsLeader.test.ts`, and `tests/game/coreRandomMedicLeader.test.ts` to reflect the new totals (14 executable + 7 passive + 1 setup = 22 implemented, 0 placeholder) and to drop placeholder-leader fixtures (only `unknown_leader_ability` remains as a placeholder validator path). Living docs updated: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12n "Cancel Leader (cCp29)" documenting the reaction / current-round suppression contract, the proactive and reactive entry points, the cancel/decline event sequences, the round-transition clear, the cancel-of-cancel block, and the AI / UI / hidden-info safety; plus a §16 row for *Emhyr var Emreis, The White Flame* pointing at §17.12n; and updates §17.12d / §17.12e to point at §17.12n for the implemented suppression interaction (replacing the prior "future cCp29 work" stubs). `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Emhyr: The White Flame's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp29)`, marks Pattern 6 IMPLEMENTED, marks Tranche 4 COMPLETE, refines Settled Product Decision §8 to mark it implemented, refreshes status counts (executable 14, passive 7 unchanged, setup 1 unchanged, placeholder 0), and appends a cCp29 change-log entry. After cCp29 the official leader matrix is complete and `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` is the empty list. |
| `cCp28` | Cluster C Phase 28 | `audit/reports/2026-05-04-cCp28-report.md` | Look Three Cards Hidden-Info Disclosure Leader (Tranche 4 first member; opens Tranche 4). Promotes `look_three_cards` leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`, making `nilfgaard.emhyr-var-emreis-emperor-of-nilfgaard` (Emhyr var Emreis: Emperor of Nilfgaard) mechanically active as the engine's first hidden-info disclosure leader. Adds the new pure helper module `src/game/core/leaderLookThreeCards.ts` exporting `LOOK_THREE_CARDS_LEADER_SOURCE_ID`, `LOOK_THREE_CARDS_REVEAL_COUNT`, `getLookThreeCardsEligibility`, and `planLookThreeCardsReveal`. The leader requires the opponent to have at least one card in hand; `getLeaderMove` emits exactly one no-target `use_leader` move (`metadata.targetRequirement === "none"`, `metadata.targetCount === revealCount`, `metadata.targetLabel === "opponent hand"`, with diagnostic `opponentHandCount` / `revealCount`). The reveal count is `min(3, opponent hand length)`. RNG advances only when the opponent has more than three cards (length 1-3 reveals the full hand without invoking RNG and leaves `state.rng.state` unchanged); for length > 3 the helper uses `createSeededRngFromState` + `shuffleWithRng` to select three cards uniformly without replacement and returns them in the opponent's natural hand order. `executeLeader` clones state, advances RNG only if subset selection happened, emits `ability_triggered`, `opponent_hand_revealed { reason: "look_three_cards", seatId, opponentSeatId, cardIds, sourceIds }`, `ability_resolved.revealed_opponent_hand`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true` (the leader is consumed at `UseLeader`), emits `leader_used`, and opens an acknowledgement prompt: `kind === "choose_option"`, `stage === "opponent_hand_reveal"`, single option `look-three-cards:acknowledge` with `target.kind === "none"`, `context.revealedCardIds` carries the chosen opponent hand card IDs. `currentTurn` stays on the acting seat while the prompt is pending. Acknowledgement (`ChoosePromptOption { optionId: "look-three-cards:acknowledge" }`) emits `prompt_resolved`, clears `state.pendingPrompt`, and the outer command path hands off the turn through `handoffTurn`. After acknowledgement, no `MatchState` field retains the reveal — the snapshot lived only in `pendingPrompt.context.revealedCardIds`. Revealed cards are not moved out of the opponent's hand and do not trigger their abilities. Adds the new `GameEvent` variant `opponent_hand_revealed` (internal/raw event log only — hidden-info-safe summaries render counts only), the new `PendingPromptTarget` variant `{ kind: "none" }`, the new `PendingPromptStage` value `"opponent_hand_reveal"`, the new `PendingPromptContext.revealedCardIds` field, and new `UseLeaderMove.metadata` diagnostic fields `opponentHandCount` / `revealCount`. Hidden-info safety preserved: `getPromptMoves` returns `[]` to the non-acting seat for the acknowledgement prompt; AI seat observation surfaces the revealed cards on the new `PendingPromptSummary.revealedCards` field to the prompt owner only and `pendingPrompt: null` to the wrong seat; safe simulation export exposes prompt-local refs `revealed_opponent_hand_<index>` (via the new `SafePromptState.revealedCards` field) to the acting perspective only and `pendingPrompt: null` to the opponent perspective; recent activity / event summaries render count-only labels ("Human looked at 3 opponent hand cards"). UI ships a one-time `authentic-look-three-cards-dialog` modal for human-owned reveal prompts (with `authentic-look-three-cards-card` and `authentic-look-three-cards-ack` test IDs) and CSS hooks under the existing `authentic-match` namespace via the new `buildLookThreeCardsRevealViewModel` view-model helper; AI-owned reveal prompts continue to surface "AI resolving prompt from legal moves" without exposing card faces. The new selector `selectEngineLookThreeCardsReveal` exposes the revealed cards as engine view models only when the human seat owns the pending acknowledgement prompt. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 12 to 13 (adds Emperor of Nilfgaard); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 20 to 21; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 2 to 1 (removes `look_three_cards`, leaving only `cancel_leader`). Adds 43 focused engine tests in `tests/game/coreLookThreeCardsLeader.test.ts` covering metadata promotion, manifest accounting (counts 13 / 7 / 1 / 21 / 1), helper outcomes (eligibility gates, hand-size 0/1/2/3/4+ reveal selection, no-RNG for ≤3 hand sizes, RNG advance for >3, opponent-hand-order preservation, determinism, missing instance / missing source defensive behavior), legal-move integration (one no-target move with correct metadata, no move on empty / used / wrong phase / wrong turn / passed / prompt pending), command execution (rejects non-`none` target without mutation, rejects empty opponent hand without consuming leader, full event sequence, `leaderUsed = true` at `UseLeader`, no turn handoff while prompt pending, prompt shape with `context.revealedCardIds`, repeated `UseLeader` is illegal, no card movement for revealed cards, no card abilities triggered, off-owner cards in opponent hand are eligible), acknowledgement (clears prompt and hands off, no reopenable snapshot retained, second `UseLeader` is illegal post-acknowledgement, wrong seat / invalid option ID rejection), hidden-info contract (`getPromptMoves` only emits to acting seat, `buildSeatObservation` includes revealed cards only for prompt owner and `pendingPrompt: null` for opponent, `buildSafeSimulationObservation` exposes `revealed_opponent_hand_<n>` refs only to acting perspective and no revealed instance IDs in opponent perspective), AI-owned prompts (human seat sees no prompt moves and `pendingPrompt: null` in observation while AI seat sees its own revealed cards), and a regression that other implemented leaders still emit their `use_leader` moves. Adds 6 focused UI tests in `tests/components/gwent/authenticMatchViewModel.test.ts` covering the new `buildLookThreeCardsRevealViewModel` helper. Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 1 entry — `cancel_leader` — executable list grows to 13 with Emperor of Nilfgaard, implemented union grows to 21, ability metadata block adds `look_three_cards`); updates `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts` (`unsupportedLeaderAbilityCounts.look_three_cards` is now `undefined`; new sentinel `unsupportedLeaderAbilityCounts.cancel_leader > 0`); updates `tests/game/coreDiscardDrawLeader.test.ts`, `tests/game/coreDrawExtraCardLeader.test.ts`, and `tests/game/coreRandomMedicLeader.test.ts` placeholder-list and manifest-count assertions to reflect cCp28 totals (13 / 7 / 1 / 21 / 1); updates `tests/components/gwent/authenticDeckBuilderViewModel.test.ts` placeholder-leader fixtures to point at `cancel_leader` (the only remaining placeholder Nilfgaard leader is now Emhyr: The White Flame); updates `tests/components/gwent/authenticMatchViewModel.test.ts` placeholder-status fixture to point at Emhyr: The White Flame (`cancel_leader`). Updates living docs: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12m "Look Three Cards Leader (cCp28)" documenting the hidden-info disclosure contract, RNG policy, one-time modal contract, no-trigger contract for revealed cards, and the hidden-info safety boundaries; plus a §16 row for *Emhyr var Emreis, Emperor of Nilfgaard* pointing at §17.12m. `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Emperor of Nilfgaard's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp28)` with the full implemented contract, marks Pattern 5 IMPLEMENTED with one-time-modal details replacing the older persistent-observation guidance, marks Tranche 4 with `look_three_cards` delivered (only `cancel_leader` remaining), refines Settled Product Decisions §6 and §7 to mark them implemented, refreshes status counts (executable 13, passive 7 unchanged, setup 1 unchanged, placeholder 1), and appends a cCp28 change-log entry. After cCp28, only `cancel_leader` (Pattern 6) remains placeholder. |
| `cCp27` | Cluster C Phase 27 | `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp27-report.md` | Discard Two Draw One Multi-Stage Prompt Leader (Tranche 3 second member; Tranche 3 COMPLETE). Promotes `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck` leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`, making `monsters.eredin-destroyer-of-worlds` (Eredin: Destroyer of Worlds) mechanically active as the engine's first true **active multi-stage prompt** leader. Adds the new pure helper module `src/game/core/leaderDiscardDraw.ts` exporting `DISCARD_TWO_DRAW_ONE_LEADER_SOURCE_ID`, `MIN_DISCARD_COUNT`, `MAX_DISCARD_COUNT`, `getDiscardDrawEligibility`, `buildDiscardSelectionOptions`, and `buildDeckDrawOptions`. The leader requires at least one card in hand and one card in deck; `getLeaderMove` emits exactly one no-target `use_leader` move (`metadata.targetRequirement === "future_prompt"`, `metadata.targetLabel === "hand then deck"`, with diagnostic `discardMin: 1`, `discardMax: 2`, `handCount`, `deckCount`). Stage 1 prompt is `kind === "choose_card_set"`, `stage === "discard_selection"`, with one option per legal 1-card discard followed by every 2-card combination in deterministic acting-seat hand order (option IDs `discard-draw:discard:<cardId>` and `discard-draw:discard:<cardIdA>+<cardIdB>`). Stage 1 resolution moves selected cards from hand to acting discard with `card_moved.reason === "leader_discard_for_draw"`, does **not** trigger battlefield discard effects (no Summon, Avenger, Medic, Spy, Scorch, weather, Muster, Berserker, Mardroeme), does **not** consume the leader, and immediately opens stage 2 (`kind === "choose_card"`, `stage === "deck_draw_selection"`, `context: { discardedCardIds }`) with one option per remaining acting-seat deck card in deck order (option IDs `discard-draw:draw:<cardId>`). Stage 2 resolution moves the chosen card from deck to acting hand with `card_moved.reason === "leader_draw_from_deck"`, sets `controller` to the acting seat (preserves `owner`), shuffles the remaining acting deck through `createSeededRngFromState` + `shuffleWithRng` (RNG advances only when remaining deck length ≥ 2; deck length 0 or 1 is a deterministic no-op), emits `deck_shuffled` with `reason === "leader_discard_draw"` (distinct from cCp23's `"leader_shuffle_into_deck"`), `prompt_resolved`, `ability_resolved.discarded_and_drew_card`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true`, emits `leader_used`, clears `state.pendingPrompt`, and hands off the turn through `handoffTurn`. Drawn cards do **not** trigger their abilities. Adds new `card_moved.reason "leader_discard_for_draw"` and `"leader_draw_from_deck"`, and `deck_shuffled.reason "leader_discard_draw"`. Extends `LegalMoveTarget` with two new acting-seat-only target kinds: `card_instance_set` (stage 1, with `cardIds: [cardIdA] | [cardIdA, cardIdB]`) and `deck_card_instance` (stage 2, with `cardId`). Extends `PendingPromptTarget` with the matching prompt-option shapes, adds `PendingPromptStage` discriminator, and introduces optional `stage` and `context` fields on `PendingPrompt`. Adds `"choose_card_set"` to the `PendingPrompt.kind` union. `getPromptMoves` returns `[]` to the non-acting seat for both stages (hidden-info safety). Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 11 to 12 (adds Destroyer of Worlds); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 19 to 20; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 3 to 2 (removes `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck`, leaving `cancel_leader` and `look_three_cards`). Adds 49 focused tests in `tests/game/coreDiscardDrawLeader.test.ts` covering metadata promotion and manifest accounting (counts 12 / 7 / 1 / 20 / 2), helper outcomes (eligibility gates, selection ordering, deck option ordering, missing-instance and missing-source defensive skipping), legal-move integration (no move on empty hand / empty deck / passed seat / leader used / wrong seat / pending prompt; one move on valid eligibility with correct metadata diagnostics), stage 1 prompt opening (correct prompt shape with stage / context / option count, ability_triggered + prompt_opened events without card_moved or leader_used, currentTurn unchanged, seat.leaderUsed false), stage 1 resolution (single-card and two-card moves with `leader_discard_for_draw` reason, off-owner `owner` preservation with `controller` reset, no Medic/Scorch/Summon triggers, prompt_resolved + new prompt_opened for stage 2 without leader consumption or handoff), stage 2 resolution (move from deck to hand with `leader_draw_from_deck` reason / controller / owner / RNG advance / `deck_shuffled` with `leader_discard_draw` reason / `ability_resolved.discarded_and_drew_card` / `leader_used` / handoff / RNG no-op for deck length 0 or 1 / no card_played / no Medic on draw), rejection paths (wrong seat, invalid option ID, stale hand card, stale deck card, non-`none` UseLeader target, empty hand, empty deck, prompt pending), legal prompt move shapes for both stages (`card_instance_set` with own side / acting seat for stage 1; `deck_card_instance` with own side / acting seat for stage 2; non-acting seat sees no prompt moves), and regression guards (cCp22 restore-discard own-side `card_instance` shape preserved, cCp24 draw-opponent-discard opponent-side `card_instance` shape preserved, Foltest Lord Commander `clear_weather` still emits its no-target `use_leader` move, no `Math.random` introduced in `src/game/core`). Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 2 entries — `cancel_leader`, `look_three_cards` — executable list grows to 12 with Destroyer of Worlds, implemented union grows to 20, sanity assertion that `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` does not contain `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck`); updates `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts` (`unsupportedLeaderAbilityCounts.discard_two_draw_one_from_deck` is now `undefined`; new sentinel `look_three_cards.unsupported > 0`); updates `tests/game/coreDrawExtraCardLeader.test.ts` and `tests/game/coreRandomMedicLeader.test.ts` placeholder-list and manifest-count assertions to reflect cCp27 totals (12 / 7 / 1 / 20 / 2). Updates living docs: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12l "Discard Two Draw One Leader (cCp27)" documenting the active multi-stage contract, eligibility gates, deterministic discard option ordering, two-stage prompt transaction, no-trigger contracts for discarded and drawn cards, deck-shuffle event with the new reason, hidden-info safety boundary; plus a §16 row for Eredin: Destroyer of Worlds; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Destroyer of Worlds's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp27)`, replaces predicted-behavior fields with the implemented contract, marks Pattern 3 IMPLEMENTED, marks Tranche 3 COMPLETE, refines Settled Product Decision §4 to mark it as implemented, refreshes status counts (executable 12, passive 7 unchanged, setup 1 unchanged, placeholder 2), and appends a cCp27 change-log entry. The existing generic `PromptPanel` renders the new `choose_card_set` and `choose_card` options as labelled buttons (`Discard <card>`, `Discard <card A> + <card B>`, `Draw <card> from deck`); no new modal or card-tile UI was needed. AI heuristic policy is unchanged but compatible: `seatObservation` exposes per-option `targetStrength` for stage 1 (sum of selected hand-card strengths) and stage 2 (chosen deck card's catalog strength), so the heuristic can rank options deterministically. Simulation export observation surfaces the acting seat's own deck card to the acting perspective only via a prompt-local safe ref `own_deck_option_<index>` so raw deck instance IDs never appear in the safe export; action encoding falls back to `none` for the new target kinds, preserving the existing safe-action target union. No UI route, layout, AI strategy tuning, simulation engine logic, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes. Browser smoke (`npm run ci:browser`) was skipped because no UI route, layout, or CSS changed; only TypeScript-only UI compatibility helpers (`playMoveHelpers.describePlayTarget`, `engineShellViewModels.TARGET_GROUP_LABELS`) were extended for exhaustiveness. |
| `cCp26` | Cluster C Phase 26 | `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp26-report.md` | Draw Extra Card Setup Leader (Tranche 3 first member). Promotes `draw_extra_card` leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented` in `src/game/catalog/abilities.ts`, making `scoiatael.francesca-findabair-daisy-of-the-valley` (Francesca Findabair: Daisy of the Valley) mechanically active as a **setup-time initial hand-size modifier**. Adds a new pure helper module `src/game/core/leaderSetup.ts` exporting `DRAW_EXTRA_CARD_LEADER_SOURCE_ID`, `BASE_INITIAL_HAND_SIZE`, and `getInitialHandDrawCountForLeader({ leaderSourceId, catalogLeaders, baseDrawCount? })`. The helper looks up the leader by source ID and returns `baseDrawCount + 1` (default 11) only when the resolved leader's `ability` is `draw_extra_card`; otherwise it returns the base count. Missing catalog entries, other abilities, or any inconsistent setup input fall back to the base count. The helper is faction-agnostic and name-agnostic; it switches purely on the catalog ability ID. Updates `src/game/core/setup.ts` to consume the helper-derived per-seat draw count instead of the hard-coded `10`. The setup pipeline order is otherwise unchanged: instantiate leader → instantiate main deck and side deck → emit `deck_instantiated` → shuffle main deck via existing `createSeededRng(seed)` + `shuffleWithRng` flow → compute per-seat draw count via the helper → slice the first N cards into hand → emit `card_moved.reason === "initial_draw"` per drawn card → emit `initial_hand_drawn` for the seat → roll initial turn through the existing RNG path → set phase to `mulligan`. The Daisy seat's setup is byte-for-byte deterministic from seed, deck list, and ordinal layout. When two configs differ only in leader identity, Daisy's first 10 hand cards exactly match the equivalent non-Daisy seat's first 10 hand cards; Daisy's 11th hand card is the next card that would otherwise have remained at the deck top of the non-Daisy seat. The mulligan budget remains capped at two redraws — `seat.mulligansUsed` starts at 0 and the standard one-card mulligan flow caps total redraws at 2; legal mulligan moves naturally include 1 keep-hand + 11 single-card mulligan options on the first tick. The leader emits no `use_leader` legal move at any phase, never sets `seat.leaderUsed`, and never emits `leader_used`; manual `UseLeader` continues to reject through the existing unsupported-leader path without state mutation. The 11th card is never chosen by prompt, kind, faction, strength, row, or ability — it is whatever the deterministic shuffle put on top. Tiny test decks shorter than 11 cards do not throw solely because of `draw_extra_card`; the existing `slice` behavior draws available cards (e.g. a 4-card deck produces a 4-card hand). No `Math.random` is introduced. Setup events reuse existing types with no new event type required. Updates `src/game/core/index.ts` to export the new helper module. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest` (in `src/data/catalog/leaders/official-promotion.ts`): introduces a new readonly bucket `implementedSetupLeaderSourceIds` (currently 1 entry: `scoiatael.francesca-findabair-daisy-of-the-valley`) distinct from `executableLeaderSourceIds` (unchanged at 11) and `implementedPassiveLeaderSourceIds` (unchanged at 7); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` becomes the sorted union of all three (now 11 + 7 + 1 = 19); `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 4 to 3 (removes `draw_extra_card`, leaving `cancel_leader`, `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck`, `look_three_cards`). The `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest` interface gains the new `implementedSetupLeaderSourceIds` field. Adds 40 focused tests in `tests/game/coreDrawExtraCardLeader.test.ts` covering metadata promotion and manifest accounting (counts 11 / 7 / 1 / 19 / 3, Daisy in setup-time set / not in executable / not in passive, `draw_extra_card` removed from placeholders), helper outcomes (Daisy returns 11 / non-Daisy returns 10 / missing leader returns 10 / custom `baseDrawCount` honored / ability-not-source-ID gating via synthetic catalog), initial draw behavior (Daisy seat draws 11 with deck shrunk by 11 / opposing non-Daisy still 10 / two-Daisy seats both 11 / `initial_hand_drawn` for Daisy carries 11 IDs / 11 `card_moved.reason === "initial_draw"` events / hand vs deck zone placement / `leaderUsed` stays false / no `leader_used` event / phase remains `mulligan` after setup / `mulligansUsed` starts at 0), determinism (same-seed identical state and events / different seeds may differ / Daisy first-10 = non-Daisy first-10 hand for the same seed and deck list / Daisy 11th = non-Daisy post-draw deck top / no `Math.random` in `src/game/core`), legal moves (12 mulligan options for Daisy on first tick — 1 keep + 11 single-card / two-redraw cap unchanged after both mulligans complete / no `use_leader` move at playing phase / manual `UseLeader` rejects without setting `leaderUsed`), regression (default Northern Realms vs Nilfgaard still draws 10/10 / both seats `leaderUsed` false / Daisy catalog entry still carries `draw_extra_card` / tiny <11-card test deck does not throw / no card lands in setup discard for either seat / opponent hand IDs are not exposed through Daisy's setup events). Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 3 entries — `cancel_leader`, `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck`, `look_three_cards` — implemented union grows to 19 with Daisy, new `implementedSetupLeaderSourceIds` assertion); updates `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts` (`unsupportedLeaderAbilityCounts.draw_extra_card` is undefined); updates `tests/game/coreRandomMedicLeader.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 3 entries, retains-Tranche-2-totals test now also asserts setup-time bucket size 1 and combined union total 19). Updates living docs: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12k "Draw Extra Card Setup Leader (cCp26)" documenting the setup-time classification, draw-11 contract, top-of-shuffled-deck behavior, mulligan-budget invariance, no-`use_leader` / no-`leader_used` / no-`leaderUsed` contract, hidden-info safety, manifest classification, and the determinism / robustness invariants; updates the §16 Specific Card FAQ with a new row for *Francesca Findabair, Daisy of the Valley*; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Daisy of the Valley's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp26, setup-time)`, replaces predicted-behavior fields with the implemented contract, adds a new "Implemented setup-time leaders" table row, marks Pattern 7 IMPLEMENTED, marks Tranche 3 IN PROGRESS with `draw_extra_card` delivered and `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck` recommended next, refines status counts (executable 11 unchanged, passive 7 unchanged, setup 1 new, placeholder 3), and appends a cCp26 change-log entry. The setup helper lives in its own module per spec recommendation rather than embedded inline in `setup.ts`. AI heuristic policy is unchanged. Hidden-info safety preserved: hand-size disclosure is already part of the public observation surface; no new public observation, UI label, AI observation, recent-activity summary, or simulation export field. No UI route, layout, AI policy tuning, simulation engine, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes. |
| `cCp24` | Cluster C Phase 24 | `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp24-report.md` | Draw Opponent Discard Active Leader (Tranche 2 fifth member). Promotes `draw_opponent_discard` leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`, making `nilfgaard.emhyr-var-emreis-the-relentless` (Emhyr var Emreis: The Relentless) mechanically playable as an active one-shot leader. Settles the cCp18 §C-2 conflict in favor of the catalog (catalog wins; printed-rulebook deck-tutor wording is superseded). Adds the pure helper `getOpponentDiscardDrawCandidates({ state, seatId, catalogCards })` in the new module `src/game/core/leaderOpponentDiscardDraw.ts` that returns the **opponent's** discard cards in opponent discard order, skipping missing instances and missing catalog sources. Eligibility is broad: units, heroes, specials, weather, side-deck-only / generated, and off-owner cards (acting-seat-owned cards physically in the opponent discard pile). `getLeaderMove` emits exactly one no-target `use_leader` move (`target.kind === "none"`, `metadata.targetRequirement === "future_prompt"`, `metadata.targetCount === candidates.length`, `metadata.targetLabel === "opponent discard"`) when the opponent discard is non-empty; empty opponent discard emits no legal `use_leader` move (even if own discard has cards). `executeLeader` opens a `pendingPrompt` (`kind === "choose_card"`, `abilityId === "draw_opponent_discard"`, one option per candidate keyed `draw-opponent-discard:<cardId>` with label `"Draw <card name> from opponent discard"`, `target.kind === "card_instance"` without `row`) **without** consuming the leader, emits `ability_triggered` and `prompt_opened`, leaves `seat.leaderUsed === false`, and keeps `currentTurn` on the acting seat while the prompt is pending. `getPromptMoves` selects target side by `prompt.abilityId`: `side: "opponent"` with `seatId: opponentSeat` for `draw_opponent_discard`, `side: "own"` for cCp22 restore prompts (preserved). Medic row-bearing targets unchanged. `ChoosePromptOption` validates the chosen card is still in the opponent's discard pile (rejects with `EngineRuleError` if not — leader untouched), moves it from opponent discard to acting hand with the new `card_moved.reason === "leader_draw_opponent_discard_to_hand"`, sets the chosen card's `controller` to the acting seat (leaving `owner` unchanged), emits `prompt_resolved`, `ability_resolved.drew_opponent_discard`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true`, emits `leader_used`, clears the pending prompt, and hands off the turn. Drawn cards do **not** resolve their abilities (no `card_played`, no Medic chain, no Scorch, no on-play weather effect) — they enter hand and behave normally only if played later. Off-owner cards in opponent discard are eligible; the chosen card moves to acting hand with `owner` preserved and `controller` reset to acting. Adds the new movement reason `"leader_draw_opponent_discard_to_hand"` to the engine `card_moved` event union in `src/game/core/types.ts`; updates the `PendingPromptOption.target.row` comment to mention cCp24 prompts also omit `row`. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 10 to 11 (adds `nilfgaard.emhyr-var-emreis-the-relentless`); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 16 to 17; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 6 to 5 (removes `draw_opponent_discard`). | `cCp25` | Cluster C Phase 25 | `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp25-report.md` | Random Medic Passive Leader (Tranche 2 sixth and final member; Tranche 2 complete). Promotes `random_medic` leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented` in `src/game/catalog/abilities.ts`, making `nilfgaard.emhyr-var-emreis-invader-of-the-north` (Emhyr var Emreis: Invader of the North) mechanically active as a **whole-match passive Medic mutation**. Settles the cCp18 §C-3 conflict in favor of the Medic-mutation reading (settled product decision §5); the catalog ID `random_medic` is preserved while the printed-rulebook "random Special replay from discard" wording is superseded. Adds a new pure helper module `src/game/core/leaderRandomMedic.ts` exporting `hasRandomMedicPolicyForSeat({ state, seatId, catalogLeaders })` (returns `true` only when the seat's `leaderSourceId` is Invader of the North and, when catalog leaders are provided, the leader's catalog ability is `random_medic`), `getRandomMedicCandidates({ state, seatId, catalogCards })` (returns the acting seat's own-discard non-hero unit cards in discard order, with the precomputed first playable row from the canonical `close → ranged → siege` ordering and the `boardSeat` precomputed by Spy lookup), and `chooseRandomMedicCandidate({ candidates, rngSeed, rngState })` (deterministic single-pick via `createSeededRngFromState`; returns `{ candidate: null }` for empty input, the only candidate without advancing RNG for a single-input set, and a uniformly chosen candidate with the new RNG state for multi-input sets). Wires the policy through `src/game/core/abilities.ts`: `resolveMedic` now receives `catalogCards` / `catalogLeaders` from `resolveCardAbilities`, checks `hasRandomMedicPolicyForSeat` for non-hero Medic sources, and on a match builds candidates, advances RNG only on a real multi-candidate roll, and routes through a new shared internal `placeMedicRevival` helper. The new helper handles `controller` reset to acting seat, `card_moved.reason === "medic_revive"`, `card_played` emission, the medic `ability_resolved` event (with caller-provided `outcome`), recursive `resolveCardAbilities` for the revived card, and `settleMardroemeRow` after placement; the prompt-based `medic_revive` resolution path now reuses this helper so the prompt and random flows cannot diverge on Spy placement, controller reset, `card_played`, child ability resolution, or Mardroeme settlement. The new `ability_resolved` outcome `"random_revived_card"` distinguishes the random path from the prompt-based `"revived_card"`; no `prompt_opened` / `prompt_resolved` events are emitted for the random path. Hero Medic sources are unaffected by the policy and continue to use the existing player-choice `medic_revive` prompt (Hero immunity, §17.1). The chain recurses: a random-revived non-hero Medic re-runs the random policy until candidates are empty. The leader emits no `use_leader` legal move, never sets `seat.leaderUsed`, and never emits `leader_used`; manual `UseLeader` rejects through the existing `assertLegal` / unsupported-leader path without state mutation. Updates `src/game/core/index.ts` to export the new helper module. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `implementedPassiveLeaderSourceIds` grows from 6 to 7 (adds `nilfgaard.emhyr-var-emreis-invader-of-the-north`); `executableLeaderSourceIds` stays at 11 (the leader is passive, not executable); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 17 to 18; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 5 to 4 (removes `random_medic`). Adds 33 focused tests in `tests/game/coreRandomMedicLeader.test.ts` covering metadata promotion and manifest accounting (counts 11 / 7 / 18 / 4, Invader in passive set / not in executable set, `random_medic` removed from placeholders), `hasRandomMedicPolicyForSeat` (true for Invader, false for The Relentless), `getRandomMedicCandidates` (empty discard / single non-hero unit with canonical row / hero-special-weather kind exclusion / opponent-discard exclusion / side-deck-only generated inclusion / off-owner non-hero unit inclusion with Spy boardSeat flip / stale missing-instance / missing-catalog-source skip / multi-row card emits one candidate with `close` row preference / discard-insertion order preservation), legal-move passive contract (no `use_leader` move emitted regardless of own discard / manual `UseLeader` rejects without leader consumption / The Relentless `draw_opponent_discard` active leader still emits its move), random resolution (no_targets path with no RNG advance / single-candidate revive without RNG advance / multi-candidate deterministic pick with RNG advance / Spy placement on opponent board with Spy draw / off-owner card revival with `owner` preserved and `controller` reset to acting / Muster chain after revival / Mardroeme transform of revived Berserker through `settleMardroemeRow` / no `prompt_opened` or `prompt_resolved` events), Medic chaining (chain recurses through random policy and ends cleanly with `no_targets` when discard empties, no pending prompt left), and regression guards (non-Invader leader still opens the `medic_revive` prompt / hero Medic source under Invader still opens the prompt via a test-local hero Medic fixture without advancing RNG / cCp22 restore-to-hand and cCp24 draw-opponent-discard prompts still return cards to hand only and do not trigger Medic). Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 4 entries — `cancel_leader`, `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck`, `draw_extra_card`, `look_three_cards` — passive set grows to 7 with Invader, implemented set grows to 18, sanity assertion that `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` does not contain `random_medic`). Updates living docs: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12j "Random Medic Passive Leader (cCp25)" documenting the passive whole-match classification, non-hero Medic source scope, hero Medic source exception, own-discard non-hero unit candidate filter, random selection / RNG advancement contract (no advance for zero or single candidates), deterministic row fallback (`close → ranged → siege`) for multi-row units, Spy placement / draw behavior, Medic chain behavior (finite, recurses through the policy), and the no-`use_leader` / no-`leader_used` / no-`leaderUsed` contract; updates the §16 Specific Card FAQ table with a new row for *Emhyr var Emreis, Invader of the North* pointing at §17.12j; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Invader of the North's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp25, passive)`, replaces the stale "Pick a random Special Card" body text with the settled Medic-mutation contract, renames Pattern 8 from "Random Special Replay From Discard" to "Passive Random Medic Mutation" and marks it IMPLEMENTED, adds an implemented passive table row for Invader of the North, marks Tranche 2 COMPLETE, marks §C-3 SETTLED & IMPLEMENTED while preserving historical conflict context, refines the Engine and UI Implications row 8, and appends a cCp25 change-log entry. The mutation is wired into the Medic resolution path only; the existing prompt path now goes through the same shared placement helper, ensuring the two flows cannot diverge on Spy placement, controller reset, `card_played`, child ability resolution, or Mardroeme settlement. AI-safe under `legal-heuristic-v0` because no new prompt is emitted; the heuristic continues to value the Medic source itself. Hidden-info safety: own discard is already public, and the event log only reveals the actually revived public card. | `cCp23` | Cluster C Phase 23 | `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp23-report.md` | Shuffle Discards Into Decks Active Leader (Tranche 2 fourth member). Promotes `shuffle_discards_into_decks` leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`, making `skellige.crach-an-craite` (Crach an Craite) mechanically playable as an active one-shot leader. Adds the pure helper `getDiscardRecyclePlan({ state })` in the new module `src/game/core/leaderDiscardRecycle.ts` that returns the deterministic seat-ordered recycle plan (`seat_a` then `seat_b`), preserves discard-insertion order inside each seat plan, and skips stale missing-card entries. `getLeaderMove` emits exactly one no-target `use_leader` move (`target.kind === "none"`, `metadata.targetRequirement === "none"`, `metadata.targetCount === plan.totalCardCount`, `metadata.targetLabel === "discard piles"`) when at least one discard pile is non-empty; both-empty discards emit no legal move (per settled product decision §10). `executeLeader` rejects any non-`none` target, rejects an empty plan, then clones state, emits `ability_triggered`, processes affected seats in stable order through a single `SeededRng` instance built from `state.rng.seed` / `state.rng.state`. Per affected seat: every planned card is moved from discard to that seat's deck with `card_moved.reason === "leader_shuffle_into_deck"`, the moved card's `controller` is reset to the destination deck seat (with `owner` unchanged), then `shuffleWithRng` shuffles the seat's full deck and `deck_shuffled.reason === "leader_shuffle_into_deck"` is emitted. Empty-discard seats are not shuffled (no hidden no-op mutation) and emit no `deck_shuffled`. After all affected shuffles, `state.rng.state` is updated to the RNG's final state. The transaction emits `ability_resolved` with `outcome === "shuffled_discards"`, `leader_used`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true`, and hands off the turn. Recycled cards do **not** resolve their abilities (no `card_played`, no Medic chain, no Scorch, no on-play weather effect). Off-owner discard cards (Spies discarded after round cleanup on the opposite board side) recycle into the discard-pile seat's deck, not the original owner's deck — `controller` is reset to the destination seat, `owner` is preserved. Removed-from-game, side deck, hand, board, row horns, weather zone, and leader zone are not touched. Adds the new movement reason `"leader_shuffle_into_deck"` to the engine `card_moved` event union, and extends the `deck_shuffled.reason` union with `"leader_shuffle_into_deck"`, in `src/game/core/types.ts`. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 9 to 10 (adds Crach an Craite); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 15 to 16; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 7 to 6 (removes `shuffle_discards_into_decks`). Adds 26 focused tests in `tests/game/coreShuffleDiscardsLeader.test.ts` covering metadata promotion, manifest accounting, helper outcomes (empty plan / stable seat order / single non-empty seat / stale missing-instance entry / determinism), legal-move integration (no-move on both-empty / one-move on acting-only-discard / one-move on opp-only-discard / one-move on both-non-empty / metadata shape / no-move post-use), command execution (non-`none` target rejection / empty-discard rejection / full transaction with both seats / off-owner card handling / untouched-zone preservation / event-sequence assertion / affected-decks-only / no-trigger-on-restore), RNG determinism (state advances / identical seeds yield identical post-state / different seeds yield different orders / unaffected-seat deck unchanged), and the post-use no-leader-move contract. Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 6 entries, executable set grows to 10 with Crach an Craite, implemented set grows to 16, sanity assertion that `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` does not contain `shuffle_discards_into_decks`); updates `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts` (`unsupportedLeaderAbilityCounts.shuffle_discards_into_decks` is undefined). Updates `tests/components/gwent/authenticMatchViewModel.test.ts` (the placeholder-status fixture switched from Crach an Craite to Emhyr var Emreis: Emperor of Nilfgaard with `look_three_cards`, since Crach is now implemented). Updates living docs: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12h "Shuffle Discards Into Decks Active Leader (cCp23)" documenting the active-one-shot contract, the empty-discards rule, the affected-decks-only shuffle rule, the off-owner-card destination semantics, the deterministic RNG contract, the stable seat order, the no-trigger-on-recycle guarantee, the Skellige round-three return interaction, and the hidden-info safety, plus a §16 Crach an Craite FAQ row; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Crach an Craite's `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp23)`, adds an active-executable table row, marks Pattern 10 IMPLEMENTED, updates Tranche 2 ordering, refines the Engine and UI Implications row 10, and appends a cCp23 change-log entry. No UI route, layout, AI policy tuning, simulation engine logic, deck-builder layout, Card Studio layout, image asset, JSONL boundary, or Playwright smoke test behavior changes. Restored cards do **not** resolve their abilities (no `card_played`, no Medic chain, no Scorch, no on-play weather effect) — they return to hand and behave normally only if played later. `PendingPromptOption.target.row` is now optional (Medic continues to set `row`; the new restore prompt omits it); `getPromptMoves` emits `card_instance` targets with or without `row` depending on the prompt option. | `cCp21` | Cluster C Phase 21 | `audit/reports/2026-05-02-cCp21-report.md` | Optimize Agile Rows Active Leader (Tranche 2 second member). Promotes `optimize_agile_rows` leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`, making `scoiatael.francesca-findabair-hope-of-the-aen-seidhe` mechanically playable as an active one-shot leader. Adds a pure planning helper `planOptimizeAgileRows({ state, seatId, catalogCards, catalogLeaders })` in the new module `src/game/core/leaderOptimizeAgile.ts` that collects the acting seat's eligible own-board non-hero Agile units (heroes, opponent-side cards, and source rows < 2 are excluded), intersects their legal rows for the common destination, simulates each candidate row through `calculateScores`, filters out pure no-op rows, and returns `{ outcome: "no_eligible" \| "no_candidate_row" \| "no_executable_row" \| "auto" \| "choice", currentScore, bestScore?, eligibleCardIds, eligibleCards, candidates, bestCandidates }`. The helper is pure and deterministic, never mutates input state, and uses only the central scoring pipeline so weather, King Bran, row-horn passives, double-spies, Tight Bond, Morale Boost, and physical Horn all compose consistently. Extends `getLeaderMove` in `src/game/core/legalMoves.ts` with an `optimize_agile_rows` branch: emits no `use_leader` move on `no_eligible` / `no_candidate_row` / `no_executable_row`; emits a single no-target `use_leader` (`targetRequirement === "none"`) on `auto`; emits one board-row-targeted `use_leader` per tied row (`targetRequirement === "agile_row_choice"`) on `choice`. The new metadata fields `currentScore` / `bestScore` / `candidateRows` are added; `metadata.targetLabel` is set to `"Close Combat"` / `"Ranged Combat"` / `"Siege Combat"` so the existing cEp8 leader-choice menu renders the choice without UI changes. Extends `executeLeader` in `src/game/core/commands.ts` with an `optimize_agile_rows` branch: rejects no-eligible / no-executable plans without consuming the leader; for `auto`, accepts only `target.kind === "none"`; for `choice`, requires `target.kind === "board_row"`, acting-seat target seat, and a row contained in `bestCandidates`; rejects board-row targets that are not tied best, wrong-seat board-row targets, and no-target execution in a tie. On success, the engine clones state, emits `ability_triggered`, moves all eligible cards in deterministic board order (`close → ranged → siege`, preserving unit order), emits `card_moved` per actually-moved card with the new `reason === "leader_optimize_agile"` (cards already on the chosen destination row produce no `card_moved` event), emits `ability_resolved.moved`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true`, emits `leader_used`, and hands off the turn through the existing handoff helper. Adds the new movement reason `"leader_optimize_agile"` to the engine `card_moved` event union in `src/game/core/types.ts`. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 7 to 8 (adds `scoiatael.francesca-findabair-hope-of-the-aen-seidhe`); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 13 to 14; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 9 to 8 (removes `optimize_agile_rows`). Adds 29 focused tests in `tests/game/coreOptimizeAgileRows.test.ts` covering metadata promotion, manifest accounting, helper outcomes (`no_eligible` / hero exclusion / opponent-board-side exclusion / agile-spy test-local fixture exclusion / unique-best-by-scoring / weather-flees / horn-attracts / morale-attracts / tied-rows / no-op-excluded), legal-move integration (auto with single no-target move + ranged target row + targetCount; choice with two board-row moves + correct labels; no-move when no eligible; post-use no-emit), command execution (auto moving three cards with all events; auto skipping cards already on the chosen row; choice accepting only tied best; choice rejecting non-best / wrong-seat / no-target; rejection without state mutation), determinism, and the standard turn / pass / phase / prompt gates. Updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` (placeholder list shrinks to 8, executable set grows to 8 with Hope of the Aen Seidhe, implemented set grows to 14, sanity assertion that `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` does not contain `optimize_agile_rows`); updates `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts` (`unsupportedLeaderAbilityCounts.optimize_agile_rows` is undefined). Updates living docs: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12f "Optimize Agile Rows Active Leader (cCp21)" documenting the eligible-cards rule, common-row selection, auto-vs-tied-choice resolution, mutation/event contract, and the cEp8 menu compatibility note; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips the Hope of the Aen Seidhe `Ability metadata status` to `implemented (cCp21)`, replaces stale "row of your choice" prose with the settled auto/tie-choice contract, marks Pattern 9 IMPLEMENTED, refines Settled Product Decision §3 to "auto-place to the highest-scoring common row; if tied, expose only the tied best rows as legal player choices", updates Tranche 2 ordering, refreshes the Engine and UI Implications table row 9, and appends a cCp21 change-log entry. | `cCp20` | Cluster C Phase 20 | `audit/reports/2026-05-02-cCp20-report.md` | Double Spies Whole-Match Passive (Tranche 2 first member). Promotes `double_spies` leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`, making `monsters.eredin-breacc-glas-the-treacherous` mechanically playable as a whole-match passive scoring derivation. Adds `getDoubleSpiesPolicyBySeat(state, leaders)` in `src/game/core/scoring.ts` mirroring cCp15's `getWeatherPolicyBySeat` and cCp19's `getRowHornPolicyBySeat`; threads it through `calculateScores` with an explicit `doubleSpiesPolicyBySeat` override. Inserts the spy multiplier between the weather step and the tight-bond step so the ordering is Weather → Double Spies → Tight Bond → Morale → Horn (per spec); adds `CardScoreEntry.spyMultiplier` and `CardScoreEntry.afterSpyMultiplier` fields; adds a distinct `"leader_double_spies"` modifier marker. The multiplier is non-stacking (×2, never ×4) even if both seats somehow have the policy, applies to both board sides regardless of owner / controller, applies to future battlefield Spies as soon as they enter, and never applies to hero Spies (Hero immunity). Round resolution, gem loss, Special Scorch, unit row-Scorch (`scorch_close` / `scorch_range` / `scorch_siege`), unit-source whole-board Scorch, and Foltest row-Scorch leaders all inherit the policy automatically because they go through `calculateScores`. The leader emits no `use_leader` move, never sets `seat.leaderUsed`, and never emits `leader_used` events; manual `UseLeader` attempts raise the existing `unsupported_command` rule error without consuming the leader. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `implementedPassiveLeaderSourceIds` grows from 5 to 6 (adds Treacherous); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 12 to 13; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 10 to 9 (removes `double_spies`). | `cCp17` | Cluster C Phase 17 | `audit/reports/2026-05-01-cCp17-report.md` | Generic Summon Discard Trigger. Promoted `summon` ability metadata from `planned` to `implemented` and added the centralised pure helper `resolveSummonForCard` in `src/game/core/abilities.ts` (returns `no_summon` / `missing_link` / `missing_replacement` / `missing_origin_row` / `summoned`). Summon triggers only when a board-row source is discarded — Special Scorch (`resolveSpecialScorch`), unit row-Scorch (`resolveScorchRow`), unit-source whole-board Scorch (`resolveUnitScorchGlobal`), the Foltest `scorch_range`/`scorch_siege` leader branch in `executeLeader`, and `sweepBattlefield` round cleanup (collected before discard, resolved after the existing `board_swept` event so replacements persist into the next round, mirroring Avenger). Replacement comes from the **controlling seat's** side deck via `linkedSourceIds[0]`, lands on the source's previous board side and row, and inherits the source's controller. Widens `card_moved.reason` with `"summon_replacement"` (distinct from Avenger's `"avenger_summon"`) and `card_summoned.abilityId` from `"avenger"` to `"avenger" \| "summon"` in `src/game/core/types.ts`; existing Avenger event shape is unchanged. Replaces the on-play `ability_deferred { abilityId: "summon", reason: "planned_ability" }` emit with `ability_triggered` + `ability_resolved.armed_for_discard`, mirroring Avenger's "armed" style. Decoy bounce, Medic revival, Skellige round-three return, Monsters keep, and weather cleanup are explicit non-Summon paths because they are not discards. Replacements are summoned, not "played" — `resolveCardAbilities` is not invoked on the replacement. The on-play deferred summon emit is gone; the `emitDiscardTriggerDeferrals` helpers in `src/game/core/abilities.ts` and `src/game/core/commands.ts` no longer emit for `summon` (Avenger deferrals are preserved). Catalog promotion is unchanged: Cow / Kambi remain `avenger`, Bovine Defense Force remains ability-less, and the combined Cow/Bovine staging candidate stays blocked by its catalog-split/Avenger issues. Official porting `unsupportedAbilityCounts.summon` is now undefined automatically because the Game8 staging contains no `summon` candidates and the metadata is `implemented`. Adds `docs/gwent-rules.md` §17.10 Summon Interactions update covering trigger conditions, controller-side-deck replacement, board-side placement, missing-link/replacement/origin outcomes, Summon-vs-Avenger distinction, and the explicit non-trigger list (Decoy, Medic, Skellige round-three return, Monsters keep, weather cleanup), plus a clarifying note in §17.10b on how Avenger and Summon differ. Adds 21 focused tests in a new `tests/game/coreSummonDiscardTrigger.test.ts` covering metadata, helper outcomes, on-play armed_for_discard, non-trigger cases, trigger paths (Special Scorch, unit row-Scorch, unit whole-board Scorch, Foltest leader, round cleanup, board-side discard preservation), Avenger interaction regressions, and a wrong-target rejection. The cCp13 Summon `it.todo` in `tests/catalog/linkedAbilityInvariants.test.ts` is promoted to an active metadata-status invariant under a new `Summon discard-trigger lock (cCp17)` block. Updates `tests/catalog/catalogValidators.test.ts` to assert `summon.status === "implemented"`. One `it.todo` remains, scoped to placeholder leader tutor/restore effects (Eredin / Emhyr / etc.). No UI route, layout, AI policy, headless simulation engine, deck-builder layout, Card Studio layout, image asset, JSONL boundary, simulation export schema, or Playwright smoke test changes. |

Extended `executeLeader` to validate target kind, target seat, and target source eligibility; find the first matching catalog source in the acting deck; move that card from deck to weather with new `card_moved.reason "leader_weather"`; set its controller to the acting seat; emit `card_played` and `leader_used`; mark the leader used only after a successful pull; and hand off the turn (existing Clear Weather leader path is unchanged). Threaded the new target shape through `src/game/sim/exportTypes.ts`, `src/game/sim/actionEncoding.ts`, `src/components/game/engine/playMoveHelpers.ts`, and `src/components/game/engine/engineShellViewModels.ts`. Added `docs/gwent-rules.md` §17.12a "Weather-Pulling Leaders (cCp14)" documenting the deck-pull contract, mappings, source set, exclusion of hand/discard/weather/board copies, no-eligible-card no-op, and explicit cCp14 no-shuffle policy.
