After cCp28, only `cancel_leader` (Pattern 6) remains placeholder.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp26-report.md` (cCp26) implements `draw_extra_card` (Francesca Findabair: Daisy of the Valley) as Tranche 3's first member: a **setup-time initial hand-size modifier** that draws 11 cards instead of 10 from the top of the already-shuffled deck before mulligan opens. Adds the new pure helper module `src/game/core/leaderSetup.ts` exporting `DRAW_EXTRA_CARD_LEADER_SOURCE_ID`, `BASE_INITIAL_HAND_SIZE`, and `getInitialHandDrawCountForLeader`; updates `src/game/core/setup.ts` to consume the helper-derived per-seat draw count instead of the hard-coded `10`. Mulligan budget remains two redraws; legal mulligan moves naturally include one-card mulligan options for all 11 hand cards. The leader emits no `use_leader` move and never sets `seat.leaderUsed`. Promotes `draw_extra_card` metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: introduces a new `implementedSetupLeaderSourceIds` bucket (1 entry: Daisy of the Valley) distinct from `executableLeaderSourceIds` (unchanged at 11) and `implementedPassiveLeaderSourceIds` (unchanged at 7); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` becomes the sorted union (now 19); `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 4 to 3. Adds 40 focused tests in `tests/game/coreDrawExtraCardLeader.test.ts`; updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts`, `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts`, and `tests/game/coreRandomMedicLeader.test.ts`. Living docs updated: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12k and a §16 row for Daisy of the Valley; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Daisy's metadata status to `implemented (cCp26, setup-time)`, adds an "Implemented setup-time leaders" table row, marks Pattern 7 IMPLEMENTED, marks Tranche 3 IN PROGRESS with `draw_extra_card` delivered and `discard_two_draw_one_from_deck` recommended next, and appends a cCp26 change-log entry. No UI route, layout, AI strategy tuning, simulation engine, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp25-report.md` (cCp25) implements `random_medic` (Emhyr var Emreis: Invader of the North) as Tranche 2's sixth and final member: a **whole-match passive Medic mutation** that randomizes non-hero Medic targets. **Tranche 2 is complete after cCp25.** Settles cCp18 §C-3 in favor of the Medic-mutation reading (settled product decision §5); the catalog ID `random_medic` is preserved while the printed-rulebook "random Special replay" wording is superseded. Adds the pure helper module `src/game/core/leaderRandomMedic.ts` exporting `hasRandomMedicPolicyForSeat`, `getRandomMedicCandidates`, and `chooseRandomMedicCandidate`; wires the policy into `resolveMedic` in `src/game/core/abilities.ts` through a new shared internal `placeMedicRevival` helper that the prompt-based `medic_revive` resolution path now also consumes, ensuring the prompt and random flows cannot diverge on Spy placement, controller reset, `card_played`, child ability resolution, or Mardroeme settlement. Hero Medic sources under Invader still open the existing `medic_revive` prompt (Hero immunity, §17.1). The chain recurses until candidate set is empty; RNG advances only on a real multi-candidate roll. New `ability_resolved` outcome `"random_revived_card"` distinguishes the random path from the prompt-based `"revived_card"`; no `prompt_opened` / `prompt_resolved` events are emitted for the random path. Promotes `random_medic` metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `implementedPassiveLeaderSourceIds` grows from 6 to 7 (adds Invader of the North); `executableLeaderSourceIds` stays at 11 (passive); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 17 to 18; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 5 to 4. Adds 33 focused tests in `tests/game/coreRandomMedicLeader.test.ts`; updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts`. Living docs updated: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12j and a §16 row for Invader of the North; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Invader's metadata status to `implemented (cCp25, passive)`, renames Pattern 8 to "Passive Random Medic Mutation" and marks it IMPLEMENTED, marks Tranche 2 COMPLETE, marks §C-3 SETTLED & IMPLEMENTED, refines the Engine and UI Implications row 8, and appends a cCp25 change-log entry. The leader emits no `use_leader` move and never sets `seat.leaderUsed`. Hidden-info safety preserved: own discard contents are public; only the chosen card is revealed in the event log. No UI route, layout, AI strategy tuning, simulation engine, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp24-report.md` (cCp24) implements `draw_opponent_discard` (Emhyr var Emreis: The Relentless) as Tranche 2's fifth member: an active one-shot leader resolved through a single-step `choose_card` prompt over the **opponent's** discard pile. Settles cCp18 §C-2 (catalog wins; printed-rulebook deck-tutor wording is superseded). Adds the pure helper `getOpponentDiscardDrawCandidates({ state, seatId, catalogCards })` in `src/game/core/leaderOpponentDiscardDraw.ts`; promotes the leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`; extends `getLeaderMove` to emit one no-target `use_leader` move with `targetRequirement === "future_prompt"`, `targetCount === candidates.length`, and `targetLabel === "opponent discard"` when the opponent discard is non-empty (and no move when empty); extends `executeLeader` to open a `pendingPrompt` (`kind === "choose_card"`, one option per candidate keyed `draw-opponent-discard:<cardId>`) **without** consuming the leader and without handing off the turn. `getPromptMoves` selects target side by `prompt.abilityId`: `side: "opponent"` for `draw_opponent_discard`, `side: "own"` preserved for cCp22 restore prompts. `ChoosePromptOption` validates the chosen card is still in the opponent's discard pile, moves it from opponent discard to acting hand with the new `card_moved.reason === "leader_draw_opponent_discard_to_hand"`, sets the chosen card's `controller` to the acting seat (leaving `owner` unchanged), emits `prompt_resolved`, `ability_resolved.drew_opponent_discard`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true`, emits `leader_used`, and hands off the turn. Drawn cards do **not** resolve their abilities. Off-owner cards in opponent discard are eligible. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 10 to 11 (adds The Relentless); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 16 to 17; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 6 to 5. Adds 31 focused tests in `tests/game/coreDrawOpponentDiscardLeader.test.ts`; updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts`, `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts`, `tests/catalog/catalogValidators.test.ts`, and `tests/components/gwent/authenticDeckBuilderViewModel.test.ts`. Living docs updated: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12i and updates the §16 Emhyr: The Relentless row to the cCp24 settled contract; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips The Relentless's metadata status to `implemented (cCp24)`, marks Pattern 4 IMPLEMENTED for both members, marks §C-2 SETTLED & IMPLEMENTED, and appends a cCp24 change-log entry. Hidden-info safety preserved: opponent discard contents are public, but AI-owned prompts remain hidden from the human UI through the existing prompt-move gate. No UI route, layout, AI strategy tuning, simulation engine, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-03-cCp23-report.md` (cCp23) implements `shuffle_discards_into_decks` (Crach an Craite) as Tranche 2's fourth member: an active one-shot leader that recycles each non-empty discard pile into the same seat's deck and shuffles only the affected decks deterministically through the engine's existing seeded RNG helpers. Adds the pure helper `getDiscardRecyclePlan({ state })` in `src/game/core/leaderDiscardRecycle.ts`; promotes the leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`; extends `getLeaderMove` to emit one no-target `use_leader` move with `targetRequirement === "none"`, `targetCount === plan.totalCardCount`, and `targetLabel === "discard piles"` when at least one discard pile is non-empty (and no move when both are empty); extends `executeLeader` to recycle each affected seat's discard cards (preserving discard order, resetting `controller` to the destination seat, leaving `owner` unchanged) and shuffle each affected seat's full deck through a single `SeededRng` instance built from `state.rng.seed` / `state.rng.state`. Empty-discard seats are not shuffled (no hidden no-op mutation) and emit no `deck_shuffled`. Recycled cards do not resolve their abilities. Adds the new event reasons `card_moved.reason "leader_shuffle_into_deck"` and `deck_shuffled.reason "leader_shuffle_into_deck"`. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 9 to 10 (adds Crach an Craite); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 15 to 16; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 7 to 6. Adds 26 focused tests in `tests/game/coreShuffleDiscardsLeader.test.ts`; updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts`, `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts`, and `tests/components/gwent/authenticMatchViewModel.test.ts`. Living docs updated: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12h and a §16 row for Crach; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Crach an Craite's metadata status to `implemented (cCp23)`, adds an active-executable table row, marks Pattern 10 IMPLEMENTED, updates Tranche 2 ordering, refines Engine and UI Implications row 10, and appends a cCp23 change-log entry. Hidden-info safety preserved: post-shuffle deck order is not exposed through legal-move metadata, AI observation, or event payloads. No UI route, layout, AI strategy tuning, simulation engine, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-02-cCp22-report.md` (cCp22) implements `restore_discard_to_hand` (Eredin: Bringer of Death) as Tranche 2's third member: an active one-shot leader resolved through a single-step `choose_card` prompt over the acting seat's own discard pile. Adds the pure helper `getRestoreDiscardCandidates({ state, seatId, catalogCards })` in `src/game/core/leaderDiscardRestore.ts`; promotes the leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`; extends `getLeaderMove` to emit one no-target `use_leader` move with `targetRequirement === "future_prompt"` when the discard is non-empty (and no move when empty); extends `executeLeader` to open a `pendingPrompt` (`kind === "choose_card"`, one option per candidate keyed `restore:<cardId>`) **without** consuming the leader and without handing off the turn — `currentTurn` stays on the acting seat while the prompt is pending. `ChoosePromptOption` validates the chosen card is still in the acting seat's discard pile, moves it from discard to hand with the new `card_moved.reason === "leader_restore_discard_to_hand"`, sets the restored card's `controller` to the acting seat (leaving `owner` unchanged), emits `prompt_resolved`, `ability_resolved.restored_card`, sets `seat.leaderUsed = true`, emits `leader_used`, and hands off the turn. Restored cards do **not** resolve their abilities — they return to hand only. `PendingPromptOption.target.row` is now optional; Medic continues to set `row`, the new restore prompt omits it. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 8 to 9 (adds Bringer of Death); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 14 to 15; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 8 to 7. Adds 31 focused tests in `tests/game/coreRestoreDiscardLeader.test.ts`; updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` and `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts`. Living docs updated: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12g; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips Eredin: Bringer of Death's metadata status to `implemented (cCp22)`, adds an active-executable table row, marks Pattern 4 IMPLEMENTED, updates Tranche 2 ordering, refines Engine and UI Implications row 4, and appends a cCp22 change-log entry. The existing generic `PromptPanel` handles the new prompt with no UI layout work; AI-owned restore prompts remain hidden from the human UI. No UI route, layout, AI strategy tuning, simulation engine, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-02-cCp21-report.md` (cCp21) implements `optimize_agile_rows` (Francesca Findabair: Hope of the Aen Seidhe) as Tranche 2's second member: an active one-shot leader that auto-places eligible own-board non-hero Agile units to the common row that yields the highest acting-seat score, exposing tied best rows as legal player choices through the existing cEp8 leader-choice path. Adds the pure planning helper `planOptimizeAgileRows` in `src/game/core/leaderOptimizeAgile.ts`; promotes the leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`; extends `getLeaderMove` and `executeLeader` to consume the helper; introduces `card_moved.reason === "leader_optimize_agile"`; extends `UseLeaderMove.metadata.targetRequirement` with `"agile_row_choice"` and adds optional `currentScore` / `bestScore` / `candidateRows` fields. Candidate rows are scored before no-op filtering, so worse movable rows are not offered when the current no-op row is uniquely best. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `executableLeaderSourceIds` grows from 7 to 8 (adds Hope of the Aen Seidhe); `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 13 to 14; `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 9 to 8. Adds 31 focused tests in `tests/game/coreOptimizeAgileRows.test.ts`; updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` and `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts`. Living docs updated: `docs/gwent-rules.md` adds §17.12f; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` flips the Hope of the Aen Seidhe metadata status, marks Pattern 9 implemented, refines Settled Product Decision §3 (auto + tied-choice), and appends a cCp21 change-log entry. No UI route, layout, AI policy, simulation engine, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-02-cCp20-report.md` (cCp20) implements `double_spies` (Eredin Breacc Glas: The Treacherous) as a **whole-match passive** scoring derivation, settling Tranche 2's first member. Promotes the leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`; adds `getDoubleSpiesPolicyBySeat(state, leaders)` to `src/game/core/scoring.ts` mirroring cCp15's `getWeatherPolicyBySeat` and cCp19's `getRowHornPolicyBySeat`; threads it into `calculateScores` with an explicit `doubleSpiesPolicyBySeat` override. The spy multiplier sits between weather and tight-bond, and the modifier order is now Weather → Double Spies → Tight Bond → Morale → Horn. Adds `CardScoreEntry.spyMultiplier` / `afterSpyMultiplier` for transparency and a distinct `"leader_double_spies"` modifier marker. The multiplier is non-stacking (×2, never ×4), applies to both board sides regardless of owner / controller, applies to future Spies on the next pass, and does not affect hero Spies. All score-dependent paths inherit the policy automatically (round resolution, gem loss, Special Scorch, unit row-Scorch, unit-source whole-board Scorch, Foltest row-Scorch leaders) because they go through `calculateScores`. The leader emits no `use_leader` move, never sets `leaderUsed`, and never emits `leader_used`. `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest`: `implementedPassiveLeaderSourceIds` grows from 5 to 6, `implementedLeaderSourceIds` grows from 12 to 13, `placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` shrinks from 10 to 9. Adds 25 focused tests in `tests/game/coreDoubleSpiesPassive.test.ts` and updates `tests/data/officialLeaderPromotion.test.ts` and `tests/data/officialCatalogImport.test.ts`. Living docs updated: `docs/gwent-rules.md` §16, §17.3, and new §17.12e; `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` corrected from "snapshot at fire time" to "whole-match passive" with Pattern 2 marked implemented and the change log appended. No UI route, layout, AI policy, simulation engine, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-02-cCp19-report.md` (cCp19) implements Tranche 1 of the cCp18 leader ability matrix: passive row-wide horn-like leader effects. Promotes `double_siege`, `double_close`, and `double_ranged` leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`, makes the four affected leader records (Foltest: The Siegemaster, Eredin: Commander of the Red Riders, Francesca: Queen of Dol Blathanna, Francesca: The Beautiful) playable as passive ongoing row-horn modifiers through the pure scoring pipeline, and adds the `getRowHornPolicyBySeat` helper. Score breakdowns now use a distinct `"leader_horn"` modifier when the leader passive applies and the existing `"commanders_horn"` modifier when a physical horn applies; physical horns suppress the leader horn (no stacking). Round resolution, gem loss, Special Scorch, unit row-Scorch, and unit-source whole-board Scorch all inherit the policy through `calculateScores`. The four affected leaders emit no `use_leader` move, never set `leaderUsed`, and never emit `leader_used`. Settles the ten cCp18-blocker product decisions and records them in `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md`; corrects `docs/gwent-rules.md` §16 so Eredin: Bringer of Death owns the discard-restore row and Emhyr: The Relentless documents the `draw_opponent_discard` catalog ability; adds §17.12d documenting the row-horn passive contract. Backfills `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest.placeholderLeaderAbilityIds` to be exhaustive (10 entries) and grows `implementedPassiveLeaderSourceIds` to 5. No UI route, layout, AI policy, simulation engine, deck-builder layout, Card Studio layout, image asset, or Playwright smoke test changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-02-cCp18-report.md` (cCp18) is the remaining-leader-ability rules matrix. Documentation/audit only — no production code, catalog data, tests, UI, AI, or simulation changed. Adds `docs/leader-ability-matrix.md` covering all 22 catalog leader source records, classifying the 14 placeholder leaders by ten implementation patterns, recording 10 local-source conflicts (§C-1 through §C-10), specifying engine/legal-move/prompt/UI/hidden-info/AI/simulation implications per pattern, ranking four cCp19+ implementation tranches, and listing 10 open product questions blocking cCp19. Three conflicts were blockers for their tranche: §C-2 (Emhyr: The Relentless catalog `draw_opponent_discard` vs `docs/gwent-rules.md` §16 deck tutor), §C-1 (Eredin: Destroyer of Worlds rulebook §16 mis-attribution), and §C-4 (`optimize_agile_rows` choice vs auto wording). cCp19 settled all three. The audit recommended Tranche 1 (row-wide horn-like passives — `double_siege`, `double_close` ×2, `double_ranged`) as the lowest-ambiguity, lowest-hidden-info first cCp19 implementation, which has now landed.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-01-cEp8-report.md` (cEp8) implements the authentic match UI for multi-move leaders. Adds a pure `buildLeaderActionViewModel` helper in `src/components/gwent/matchViewModel.ts`, wires it into `AuthenticMatchScreen.tsx`, and ships a compact `authentic-leader-choice-menu` popover with required test IDs, ARIA wiring, click-outside/Escape/disable handling, and exact `UseLeader` command dispatch. Closes the gap where `play_any_weather` silently fired the first engine-sorted weather target. Adds 7 focused unit tests and one Playwright Chromium smoke. No engine rule, legal-move generation, command execution, AI policy, simulation engine, deck-builder, Card Studio, official porting, or catalog promotion changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-01-cCp17-report.md` (cCp17) implements the generic `summon` discard-trigger resolver. Promotes `summon` ability metadata from `planned` to `implemented`. Adds the pure helper `resolveSummonForCard` in `src/game/core/abilities.ts` mirroring `resolveAvengerForCard`'s shape and outcome union (`no_summon` / `missing_link` / `missing_replacement` / `missing_origin_row` / `summoned`). Summon fires only when a board-row source is discarded, with the replacement coming from the **controlling seat's** side deck via `linkedSourceIds[0]` and landing on the source's previous board side and row. Movement uses a new `card_moved.reason "summon_replacement"`; `card_summoned.abilityId` is widened from `"avenger"` to `"avenger" | "summon"`; the on-play emit is now `ability_triggered` + `ability_resolved.armed_for_discard`. Wires the resolver into Special Scorch, unit row-Scorch, unit-source whole-board Scorch, Foltest `scorch_range`/`scorch_siege` leader execution, and round cleanup (after the row sweep so replacements persist into the next round). Decoy bounce, Medic revival, Skellige round-three return, Monsters keep, and weather cleanup remain explicit non-Summon paths. Avenger semantics (Cow / Kambi) are unchanged; the combined Cow/Bovine staging candidate stays blocked by its catalog-split issues; Bovine Defense Force remains ability-less. Documents the rule in `docs/gwent-rules.md` §17.10 (with a clarifying §17.10b note). Adds 21 focused tests in `tests/game/coreSummonDiscardTrigger.test.ts`; promotes the cCp13 `it.todo` for Summon to an active metadata-status invariant; updates `tests/catalog/catalogValidators.test.ts` for the new metadata status. Catalog promotion counts and existing official-porting unsupported counts are unchanged. No UI route, layout, AI policy, simulation engine, or asset changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-01-cCp16-report.md` (cCp16) implements the two Foltest row-Scorch active leaders (`scorch_range`, `scorch_siege`). Promotes the leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`. Adds a small pure helper module `src/game/core/leaderRowScorch.ts` mapping `scorch_range`→ranged and `scorch_siege`→siege. Extends `getLeaderMove` to wrap the cCp11 `findUnitScorchRowTargets` helper and emit a no-target `use_leader` move only when at least one tied-highest non-hero unit can be destroyed. Extends `executeLeader` to destroy all tied-highest non-hero units on the mapped opponent row, fire Avenger replacement parity with Special/unit row-Scorch destruction, and mark the leader used after a successful destroy. Updates `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest.executableLeaderSourceIds` from five to seven leaders. Documents the rule in `docs/gwent-rules.md` §17.12c. Adds 16 focused tests in `tests/game/coreLeaderRowScorch.test.ts`. No UI route, layout, AI policy, simulation engine logic, or asset changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-01-cCp15-report.md` (cCp15) implements King Bran's passive `weather_half_penalty`. Promotes the leader ability metadata from `placeholder` to `implemented`. Adds `getWeatherPolicyBySeat` helper that derives `WeatherPolicy = "king_bran"` from leader identity (not faction) and wires it into `calculateScores`. Threads `catalogLeaders` through Special Scorch in `commands.ts` and row Scorch / unit-source whole-board Scorch in `abilities.ts`. Splits `OfficialLeaderPromotionManifest` into active executable, passive implemented, and combined implemented sets. Keeps `getLeaderMove` returning `[]` for King Bran so no `use_leader` move is emitted, no `leader_used` event fires, and `leaderUsed` is never set by the passive. Adds 19 focused tests in `tests/game/coreKingBran.test.ts`. Documents the rule in `docs/gwent-rules.md` §17.12b. No UI route, layout, AI policy, simulation engine logic, or asset changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-01-cCp14-report.md` (cCp14) implemented the four weather-pulling leader commands (`play_frost`, `play_fog`, `play_rain`, `play_any_weather`) with a hidden-info-safe `deck_card_source` legal target shape and a small pure helper at `src/game/core/leaderWeather.ts`.
- `audit/reports/2026-05-01-cCp13-report.md` (cCp13) is a focused implementation phase that resolves the cCp12 follow-ups: Light Longship now declares `linkedSourceIds: ["skellige.light-longship"]` and resolves as a same-source Muster; every current/official preset containing Geralt or Cirilla now ships one `neutral.roach` so `muster_roach` visibly resolves; the Arachas Behemoth one-way relationship is promoted from `it.todo` to active catalog and engine assertions; Hemdall is locked down as ability-less side-deck-only with Kambi as its sole linker; the Muster taxonomy is documented in `docs/gwent-rules.md` §17.11a (exact-source copy, symmetric family, one-way linked, non-reciprocal exceptions). The two remaining `it.todo` placeholders are scoped to the genuinely deferred generic `summon` discard-trigger resolver and placeholder leader tutor/restore effects. No engine rule logic, AI policy, simulation engine, UI, route, or asset changes.
- `audit/reports/2026-04-30-cCp12-report.md` (cCp12) is a linked-ability invariant audit and regression-coverage phase. No production engine, catalog, UI, AI, simulation, or rule documentation changes. Added `tests/catalog/linkedAbilityInvariants.test.ts` (23 active assertions + 3 `it.todo` placeholders) and `tests/game/coreLinkedAbilities.test.ts` (7 active engine regressions covering Gaunter both directions, Cerys → Shield Maiden, Nekker same-source Muster excluding board/discard, Crone multi-card group, Cow Avenger summoning Bovine, Berserker Mardroeme summoning Vildkaarl). Extended `tests/game/legacyMuster.test.ts` with explicit Darkness-never-pulls-base-Gaunter coverage. The cCp10 reverse Gaunter miss is now locked down across catalog invariants, engine regressions, and legacy regressions. Documented `it.todo` follow-ups: Arachas Behemoth reciprocal pull (currently one-way), Light Longship deferred Muster group, and the planned `summon` discard-trigger resolver. No `P0`/`P1` issues found; default Northern Realms vs Nilfgaard match and every official starter preset linked effect is covered by an active assertion or invariant.
- `audit/reports/2026-04-30-cEp5.3-report.md` (cEp5.3) implemented in-match read-only card and leader inspection. UI: `AuthenticMatchScreen` now shows a single-action `inspect` context menu and read-only inspect modal for every visible match surface (human hand, board cards, row horns, weather, discard browser cards, Medic prompt option cards, leaders). View model: `buildMatchCardInspection`/`buildMatchLeaderInspection` helpers in `matchViewModel.ts`. Engine rules, legal moves, AI policy, deck validation, and `CatalogDeckPreset.sideDeck` storage are unchanged. AI hand/deck identities remain hidden in normal routes.
- `audit/reports/2026-04-30-cEp5.2-report.md` (cEp5.2) implemented the deck-builder generated-cards UX and card inspect modal. Catalog: `neutral.bovine-defense-force` is now tagged `side_deck_only`. Deck builder: `side deck · linked replacements` rail replaced with a `generated cards` rail rendering inspectable thumbnails plus "generated by ..." labels, `buildGeneratedCardItems`/`buildDeckBuilderCardActions`/`buildDeckBuilderCardInspection` helpers, right-click context menu on pool/deck/generated cards, and a read-only inspect modal with the full card profile and same add/remove availability rules. Internal `CatalogDeckPreset.sideDeck` and validation error codes unchanged.
- `audit/reports/2026-04-30-cCp11-report.md` (cCp11) reconciled the engine and catalog with the Witcher Fandom scrape (`audit/scrapes/2026-04-30-witcher-fandom/`) and the cCp10 audit findings. Implementation phase: catalog corrections, ability metadata promotions, hero-source row effects, row-scoped unit Scorch, unit-source whole-board Scorch with self-include, shared Avenger battlefield removal replacement, and explicit Clear Weather + Skellige Storm regression coverage.
- `audit/reports/2026-04-29-cCp10-report.md` (cCp10) audited the implemented engine ruleset against `docs/gwent-rules.md`. The three P2 findings raised by cCp10 (hero Morale Boost, unit Scorch firing, Cerys Muster link) are now resolved by cCp11.

## Known Architectural Rules

- Engine legal moves are the UI/AI contract.
- React and Redux may not compute rule outcomes.
- Engine UI and adapter paths may not use legacy rule helpers or legacy AI.
- AI hidden hand details must not appear in the default human engine view.
- `docs/gwent-rules.md` is the authority for rule behavior.
- Row-occupying battlefield cards sent to discard by round cleanup or Scorch go
  to the discard pile for the side they currently occupy. This includes Spies
  on the opponent board. Global weather and non-row effect discards still use
  controller discard unless an effect says otherwise.

## Active Risks And Limits

- Browser click-through is now covered by a small Playwright Chromium smoke harness, but it is intentionally short and does not finish a full game.
- Engine UI is not default.
- `legal-heuristic-v0` is intentionally weak and deterministic; it proves legal-move AI plumbing but is not full strategic AI.
- Current catalog is incomplete by design.
- Placeholder leader/card abilities remain deferred until scoped specs implement them.
- The immediate AI action loop remains effect-driven and intentionally delay-free; shell summaries make the aftermath legible, but richer pacing remains deferred.
- Mobile layout has wrapping safeguards for controls and target groups, but final spatial board UX remains out of scope.
- Playwright browser binaries install outside the repository cache locally, so first-time local setup may require filesystem/network permission. GitHub Actions installs Chromium explicitly before running the browser gate.
- Browser selectors are stable `data-testid` hooks on visible shell regions and controls, but they are still coupled to the current button-driven smoke surface and should be revised when the final spatial board UI lands.
- Browser CI now runs an additional build plus Chromium smoke suite after `npm run ci`; runtime is still small for the current 37-test smoke suite, but browser install/download time can dominate cold CI runs.
- Headless simulation has max-step protection, but non-termination is still a risk for future policies that repeatedly choose legal no-progress moves; keep bounded runs and status checks around every simulation entrypoint.
- Prompt handling is covered by the cDp8 smoke seed through a Medic prompt, but broader prompt kinds and chained prompt-heavy games need future seed suites.
- Simulation step logs are compact and avoid full observations/state snapshots, but `commandLog`, final state, and event logs can contain hidden card instance/source IDs for replay; they are not hidden-info-safe ML export data yet.
- Replay verifies deterministic command application for the current catalog config, but it does not yet hash observations, validate every intermediate state, or promise cross-version replay compatibility.
- Policy failures are reported as structured `policy_failed` results for missing acting seats, thrown policy exceptions, no selected move, illegal selected moves, or move conversion failure; stronger policy diagnostics and illegal-action metrics belong in later simulation phases.
- Batch diagnostics are intentionally compact and hidden-info cautious by default, but `includeRawResults: true` exposes cDp8 final states and command logs for debugging; those raw results are not ML-safe export rows.
- `current-smoke-v1` is a small fixed seed suite for regression visibility, not a tournament matrix or policy evaluation ladder.
- Safe export rows are hidden-info-safe by explicit field selection plus cDp11 validation/redaction scanning, but future consumers still need file/storage policy before writing JSONL outside memory.
- Legal actions remain per-row lists rather than a fixed model action vector; downstream ML tooling must still define tensorization/masking.
- Reward placeholders are sparse terminal outcomes only; no shaped reward design or round-level training signal exists yet.
- `includeSystemActions: true` can expose simulation auto-resolver rows for diagnostics, but default policy datasets continue to exclude `resolve_round_end`.
- JSONL parsing validates reconstructed datasets and reports malformed input as structured issues, but it is not a replay checker, schema library, compression format, Python bridge, or compatibility guarantee.
- The UI handoff prototype is fixed-size and browser-global by design; Cluster E must adapt it into responsive React/TypeScript components and production styling.
- The UI handoff README has been annotated with current production paths. Treat the prototype files as visual references only; use current catalog, engine, Redux adapter, AI, and simulation paths for implementation work.
- The authentic UI foundation harness is now a development view only. The pre-game route starts configured matches, the deck-builder route edits browser-local catalog/custom decks, the Card Studio route edits browser-local custom cards/leaders, and the direct match route exercises engine commands, legal moves, prompts, and AI. cEp12 adds pointer drag/drop and card-flight for legal human hand-card plays, and cEp13 adds generated active-weather row overlays. Touch dragging, fuller Decoy choreography, and broader match animations remain deferred.
- The authentic harness depends on a Google Fonts `@import` for `EB Garamond` and `JetBrains Mono`, currently loading normal/italic EB Garamond 400/500/600/700 and JetBrains Mono 400/500/600/700. Production deployments without external font access fall back to the documented serif/monospace stacks. JetBrains Mono is intentionally opt-in through `--font-data` rather than a general label font. The `/legacy` route still references `Inter, sans-serif` from `src/styles/global.css` without importing Inter.
- Card images are still incomplete by design. The `SvgCardArt` placeholder is a deterministic fallback, not a content workflow.
- The official porting image manifest uses preferred `/images/<faction>/<kind-folder>/...` paths and records many missing preferred paths by design; Game8 image URLs are provenance only and no scraped image binaries are committed.
- The official porting review bundle is browser-local until exported. cBp4/cBp4.1 covered the non-leader promotion + combined-card cleanup; future phases must still consume the bundle for official leaders, official-faction starter decks, and side-deck authoring.
- The combined Cow/Bovine scrape candidate (`neutral.cow-bovine-defense-force`) remains deferred until Avenger lands as an implemented ability; the legacy split `neutral.cow` and `neutral.bovine-defense-force` records cover the playable form today.
- Every current catalog card and leader image now points at a committed public asset; this is covered by `tests/catalog/currentCatalogData.test.ts`.
- Dwarven Skirmisher is now one Close Combat Muster source with `deckLimit: 3`; the earlier scrape-derived ranged/close split was incorrect and is recorded only as a duplicate-resolution mapping in `officialPromotionManifest`.
- Current non-leader card ability metadata is now closed for current catalog abilities. cCp11 promoted `avenger` to `implemented` (Cow / Kambi battlefield-removal replacement), cCp17 promoted generic `summon` to `implemented` (discard-from-board replacement), and cCp31 promoted `muster_roach` to `implemented` to match the already-shipped explicit-link resolver behavior for Geralt / Cirilla -> Roach. Future custom or imported abilities still require normal metadata and engine validation.
- Side-deck authoring UI is intentionally narrow. cBp5 added linked side-deck Deck Builder support — automatic side-deck sync from `linkedSourceIds`, focused validation error codes, persistence, import/export, and a compact UI section. Free-form side-deck editing is still not supported.
- Promoted official leader art now resolves to committed public image paths after the cBp5 follow-up. Leader fallback rendering remains necessary for future custom or missing-path content.
- Active executable leader legal moves currently cover **fourteen** leader records: Foltest Lord Commander (`clear_weather`), the four cCp14 weather-pulling leaders, the two cCp16 Foltest row-Scorch leaders (`Foltest: Son of Medell` / `scorch_range`, `Foltest: The Steel-Forged` / `scorch_siege`), Francesca Findabair: Hope of the Aen Seidhe (`optimize_agile_rows`, cCp21), Eredin: Bringer of Death (`restore_discard_to_hand`, cCp22), Crach an Craite (`shuffle_discards_into_decks`, cCp23), Emhyr var Emreis: The Relentless (`draw_opponent_discard`, cCp24), Eredin: Destroyer of Worlds (`discard_two_draw_one_from_deck`, cCp27), Emhyr var Emreis: Emperor of Nilfgaard (`look_three_cards`, cCp28), and **Emhyr var Emreis: The White Flame (`cancel_leader`, cCp29)**. Implemented passive leaders total seven after cCp25 (unchanged through cCp29). Implemented setup-time leaders total one after cCp26 (unchanged through cCp29). The active executable set grew from seven (cCp16) to **fourteen (cCp29)** by adding Hope of the Aen Seidhe, Bringer of Death, Crach an Craite, The Relentless, Destroyer of Worlds, Emperor of Nilfgaard, and The White Flame. All passive effects run through scoring derivation rather than producing a clickable `UseLeader` command. **The official leader matrix is complete:** 0 leader records remain placeholder.
- Faction-specific card-back files are not present in the repository yet. Until they are added under `public/images/card-backs/`, the authentic back component falls back to synthetic faction-colored sleeves.

## Next Recommended Step

Cluster F has a playable product-facing `legal-heuristic-v1`, hidden-info-safe
decision diagnostics, deterministic benchmark artifacts, calibrated
failure-mining artifacts, a deterministic Glicko-1 rating layer for the v1
starter matrices, multi-period rating snapshots/comparisons, robust
failure-mining casebooks through cFp56, cFp58 search-readiness design, cFp59
root-profiler artifacts showing tractable benchmark root branching (robust max
legal moves 32, max target expansion 26), cFp60 known-preset sampler-readiness
artifacts, and cFp61 hidden-multiset materialization artifacts. Batch A and
Batch B remain the controlling research notes for search and evaluation-ladder
work:
`docs/research/literature/ai/decisions/2026-05-09-batch-a-search-baseline.md`
and
`docs/research/literature/ai/decisions/2026-05-09-batch-b-evaluation-ladder.md`.
The benchmark stack now has safe known-preset sampled-world construction for
valid official-starter roots, but it still does not explain the remaining cFp61
invalid roots, run PIMC, ISMCTS, rollout search, self-play, model training,
mechanics/competitive robustness probes, or product difficulty.

Next recommended sequence:

1. implement cFp62 as a sampler invalid-root casebook: add scalar-only
   diagnostics for the 404 cFp61 `insufficient_prior_remaining` roots, classify
   every invalid root exactly once, and decide whether cFp63 should refine the
   sampler or run `determinized-pimc-probe-v0` over valid roots only with
   explicit skip accounting;
2. keep hand-tuned `legal-heuristic-v1` round-one tuning paused unless a future
   public fixture justifies a narrow repair;
3. after cFp62, choose between a narrow sampler repair, random-rollout sanity
   checking, or a first benchmark-only `determinized-pimc-probe-v0` based on the
   invalid-root casebook and cFp61 valid-root readout;
4. preserve cFp52 before-fix and cFp53 post-repair debug artifacts as paired
   historical evidence;
5. keep TrueSkill-compatible schema as a later evaluation-layer option after
   Glicko-1 artifact semantics are stable across more benchmark refreshes;
6. keep ISMCTS, OSFP, NFSP, Deep CFR, ReBeL, approximate best-response probes,
   model training, and product difficulty tiers deferred until they consume the
   existing ledger/artifact foundation and the sampler/action contracts.

Pre-AI Cluster E and Cluster C follow-ups remain open and can run in
parallel with the research-foundation work:

- broader coverage backfill: Decoy + replay regression, Decoy +
  Mardroeme regression, ranged-row Berserker round-3 return, and
  Monsters keep with no eligible units;
- documentation-only follow-ups: cCp18 §C-5, §C-6, §C-8, §C-10
  (catalog description alignment, staging provenance, scrape
  coverage).

## Active Risks (cFp22 benchmark artifacts, cFp20 decision note, cFp18.3 Brown ReBeL completion, and cFp17.2 Brown Deep CFR completion)

- cFp22 is the first durable benchmark artifact layer, not a full
  tournament/rating/research platform. It should not be cited as
  evidence that Glicko, TrueSkill, Elo, approximate best response,
  ISMCTS, determinized search, self-play, model training, Python export,
  browser file export, or product difficulty tiers exist.
- cFp22 default artifacts are hidden-info safe by field selection and
  artifact tests, but the underlying cFp21 API still has
  `includeDebugResults: true` as an unsafe raw headless simulation escape
  hatch. The CLI script intentionally does not expose that option.
- cFp20 remains a decision synthesis over three completed evaluation
  annotations. It should not be cited as evidence that the rating
  system, broad tournament runner, or approximate best-response probe
  already exists.
- Batch B supports layered evaluation only with guardrails: raw match
  ledgers before ratings, stratified matchup matrices before aggregate
  leaderboards, uncertainty reporting for every rating, and explicit
  separation between outcome ratings and robustness/exploitability
  probes.
- Glicko is the recommended first local rating layer because current
  Gwent evaluation is two-player and one-agent-per-seat, but the result
  schema should remain TrueSkill-compatible for future human or
  multiplayer/team use.
- Approximate best-response remains deferred until the ledger/matrix
  foundation exists, and any future score must be reported as a lower
  bound or diagnostic, not exact exploitability.

- cFp19 is a decision synthesis over three completed annotations, not a
  runtime implementation phase. It should not be cited as evidence that
  ISMCTS, determinized search, or any product AI difficulty tier is
  already designed or ready to implement.
- Batch A supports search only with guardrails: hidden-info-safe inputs,
  explicit variant separation, Long-style determinization-risk
  measurements, branching instrumentation, budget/runtime reporting, and
  no equilibrium or "master-level" strength claims.
- Search implementation remains gated on the cFp21 ledger/matrix
  foundation plus future search-readiness measurements. Writing an
  ISMCTS implementation spec next would still skip Batch A's
  determinization-risk guardrails.

- cFp18.3 is documentation and research annotation finalization only.
